  "short_description": {
    "id": "Chupacabra adalah makhluk legendaris atau kriptid dalam cerita rakyat sebagian wilayah Amerika yang konon memangsa ternak dengan menghisap darahnya. Penampakan yang tersiar luas dimulai di Puerto Riko pada 1995.",
    "en": "In the folklore of parts of the Americas, the chupacabra is a legendary cryptid that is said to attack livestock and take their blood. Sightings that spread widely in the press began in Puerto Rico in 1995.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "chupacabra-c01",
      "chupacabra-c03",
      "chupacabra-c07"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Chupacabra atau chupacabras, secara harfiah \"penghisap kambing\", adalah makhluk legendaris atau kriptid dalam cerita rakyat sebagian wilayah Amerika. Konon ia memangsa ternak, terutama kambing, walau laporan juga menyebut serangan terhadap domba, ayam, dan hewan piaraan lain. Menurut laporan, ia membunuh dengan melubangi leher dan menghisap darah, ciri yang melekatkannya pada vampirisme. Menurut Radford, pada pertengahan 2000-an nama itu dipakai untuk koyote berkudis, rakun mati, bahkan ikan kering di New Mexico.",
      "en": "The chupacabra, or chupacabras, literally \"goat-sucker\", figures in the folklore of parts of the Americas as a legendary creature, or cryptid. It is said to hunt livestock, mainly goats, though reports also blame it for attacks on sheep, chickens and other domestic animals. Accounts say it kills by puncturing the neck and taking the blood, a trait that tied it to vampirism. By the mid-2000s, according to Radford, the name was being applied to mangy coyotes, dead raccoons and even a dried fish in New Mexico.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "chupacabra-c01",
        "chupacabra-c02",
        "chupacabra-c03",
        "chupacabra-c37"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Gambarannya sangat beragam menurut daerah. Di Puerto Riko dan sebagian besar Amerika Hispanik, ia umumnya digambarkan mirip reptil atau makhluk asing, seukuran beruang kecil, dengan deretan duri dari leher hingga pangkal ekor; saksi pertama menyebutnya berkaki dua dengan kepala lonjong mirip makhluk asing. Di Amerika Serikat bagian barat daya, ia biasanya digambarkan sebagai hewan mirip anjing tanpa bulu bertaring menonjol, dan jenis ini kemudian menjadi gambaran yang dominan. Sebuah surat kabar Puerto Riko memuat sketsa saksi itu pada 1995, dan lebih dari 200 laporan menyusul di pulau itu saja.",
      "en": "How it looks depends heavily on where the report comes from. Puerto Rico and much of Hispanic America usually picture a reptilian or alien-looking animal, roughly a small bear in size, with spines running from neck to tail base; the first eyewitness described a bipedal creature with an oblong, alien-like head. Reports from the Southwestern United States more often describe a hairless canine with prominent fangs, and this type later became the dominant image. A newspaper in Puerto Rico printed the eyewitness sketch in 1995, and over 200 reports followed on that island alone.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "chupacabra-c04",
        "chupacabra-c05",
        "chupacabra-c32",
        "chupacabra-c06",
        "chupacabra-c25",
        "chupacabra-c35",
        "chupacabra-c41"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Sosok ini dikenal sejak 1995. Pada 1975 kematian ternak di Moca, Puerto Riko, dikaitkan dengan \"el vampiro de Moca\", dan pada Maret 1995 delapan domba ditemukan mati dengan luka tusuk. Nama chupacabras dikaitkan dengan komedian Silverio Pérez, yang mencetuskannya pada 1995. Sejak itu penampakan dilaporkan dari Maine hingga Chile, termasuk Calama di Chile pada 2000 dan Argentina pada 2002. Menurut satu sumber, dalam 21 tahun sejak dugaan penampakan pertama makhluk ini pernah menjadi alien berpunggung duri, kanguru bersayap, goblin, monyet pemangsa, atau musang yang luar biasa ambisius; pada 2000 seorang peternak Nikaragua menembak sesuatu yang menyerang kambingnya, dan bangkai yang ditemukan beberapa hari kemudian ternyata canid kurus tanpa bulu. Laporan penampakan merebak di berbagai lokasi pada pertengahan 1990-an.",
      "en": "The figure is known from 1995. In 1975 livestock deaths in Moca, Puerto Rico, were tied to \"el vampiro de Moca\", and in March 1995 eight sheep were found dead with puncture wounds. The name chupacabras is credited to the comedian Silverio Pérez, who coined it in 1995. Since then sightings have been reported from Maine to Chile, including Calama in Chile in 2000 and Argentina in 2002. One source lists the forms it has taken in 21 years, from a spine-backed alien to a winged kangaroo, a goblin, a predatory monkey or an unusually ambitious mongoose; in 2000 a Nicaraguan rancher shot something attacking his goats, and the carcass found days later was a hairless, rangy canid. Reports of sightings multiplied across various locations in the mid-1990s.",

    {
      "id": "Para penyelidik mengajukan penjelasan duniawi. Radford menyimpulkan bahwa deskripsi Madelyne Tolentino didasarkan pada makhluk dalam film Species, dan nekropsi tidak menemukan korban yang kehabisan darah. Bangkai di Texas tidak mirip sosok asli Puerto Riko, dan uji DNA menunjukkan koyote atau anjing berkudis, anjing peliharaan sering membunuh dengan gigitan di leher lalu meninggalkan bangkai, dan Barry O'Connor menyimpulkan semua laporan di Amerika Serikat adalah koyote bersarkoptes. Para pemercaya, seperti Canion, tidak puas dan berkeras bahwa makhluk itu bukan koyote berkudis biasa.",
      "en": "Investigators offer earthly explanations. Radford concluded that Madelyne Tolentino's description drew on a creature in the film Species, and necropsies found no victims drained of blood. The Texas carcasses looked nothing like the original Puerto Rican figure, and DNA tests showed mangy coyotes or dogs, domestic dogs often kill with a neck bite and leave the carcass, and Barry O'Connor concluded all United States reports were coyotes with sarcoptic mange. Believers, such as Canion, were unsatisfied and insisted it was no ordinary mangy coyote.",

    "id": "Menurut Radford, El Chupacabra adalah salah satu makhluk mitos pertama yang ditemukan di era internet, dan kisahnya menyebar ke seluruh dunia, terutama ke negara berbahasa Spanyol, dalam hitungan pekan.",

  "learning_questions": [
    {
      "id": "Mengapa satu deskripsi saksi dapat memengaruhi laporan-laporan berikutnya di banyak negara?",
      "en": "Why can one eyewitness description shape later reports in many countries?"
    },
    {
      "id": "Apa yang dapat dijelaskan oleh kudis pada koyote tentang wujud chupacabra di Texas?",
      "en": "What can mange in coyotes explain about the chupacabra's form in Texas?"
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c01",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "literally meaning 'goat-sucker', from Spanish: chupa , 'sucks', and cabras , 'goats') is a legendary creature, or cryptid, in the folklore of parts of the Americas.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Chupacabra (chupacabras), secara harfiah 'penghisap kambing', dari kata Spanyol chupa 'menghisap' dan cabras 'kambing', adalah makhluk legendaris atau kriptid dalam cerita rakyat sebagian wilayah Amerika.",
        "en": "The chupacabra (chupacabras), literally 'goat-sucker', from Spanish chupa 'sucks' and cabras 'goats', is a legendary creature or cryptid in the folklore of parts of the Americas."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c02",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "It is said to prey on livestock, particularly goats, although reports also attribute attacks to sheep, chickens and other domesticated animals.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Konon ia memangsa ternak, terutama kambing, meski laporan juga menyebut serangan terhadap domba, ayam, dan hewan piaraan lain.",
        "en": "It is said to prey on livestock, particularly goats, although reports also attribute attacks to sheep, chickens and other domesticated animals."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c03",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "According to accounts, the chupacabra kills its victims by puncturing the neck and consuming their blood, a characteristic that gave rise to its enduring association with vampirism.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut laporan, chupacabra membunuh korbannya dengan melubangi leher dan menghisap darahnya, ciri yang melahirkan kaitannya yang bertahan lama dengan vampirisme.",
        "en": "According to accounts, the chupacabra kills its victims by puncturing the neck and consuming their blood, a trait that gave rise to its enduring association with vampirism."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c04",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "Descriptions of the creature vary considerably by region.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penggambaran makhluk ini sangat beragam menurut daerah.",
        "en": "Descriptions of the creature vary considerably by region."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c05",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "In Puerto Rico and much of Hispanic America, it is commonly portrayed as reptilian or extraterrestrial-looking, about the size of a small bear, and with a distinctive row of spines extending from the neck to the base of the tail.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Puerto Riko dan sebagian besar Amerika Hispanik, ia umumnya digambarkan mirip reptil atau makhluk asing, seukuran beruang kecil, dengan deretan duri dari leher hingga pangkal ekor.",
        "en": "In Puerto Rico and much of Hispanic America it is commonly portrayed as reptilian or extraterrestrial-looking, about the size of a small bear, with a row of spines from the neck to the base of the tail."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c06",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "In contrast, reports from the Southwestern United States typically describe the creature as a hairless, canine animal with pronounced fangs.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebaliknya, laporan dari Amerika Serikat bagian barat daya umumnya menggambarkannya sebagai hewan mirip anjing tanpa bulu dengan taring menonjol.",
        "en": "In contrast, reports from the Southwestern United States typically describe it as a hairless, canine animal with pronounced fangs."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c07",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "The first widely publicized sightings and descriptions of the chupacabra emerged in Puerto Rico in 1995.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penampakan dan penggambaran pertama yang tersiar luas muncul di Puerto Riko pada 1995.",
        "en": "The first widely publicized sightings and descriptions of the chupacabra emerged in Puerto Rico in 1995."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c08",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "Since then, alleged encounters have been reported throughout the Americas, ranging from Maine to Chile, as well as in countries outside the region, including Russia and the Philippines.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sejak itu, perjumpaan yang diklaim dilaporkan di seluruh benua Amerika, dari Maine hingga Chile, juga di luar kawasan itu seperti Rusia dan Filipina.",
        "en": "Since then, alleged encounters have been reported throughout the Americas, from Maine to Chile, and also outside the region, in places such as Russia and the Philippines."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c09",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "The name Chupacabras literally translates to 'goat-sucker', deriving from the Spanish words chupar ('to suck') and cabras ('goats').",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Chupacabras secara harfiah berarti 'penghisap kambing', dari kata Spanyol chupar ('menghisap') dan cabras ('kambing').",
        "en": "The name Chupacabras literally translates to 'goat-sucker', from the Spanish words chupar ('to suck') and cabras ('goats')."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c10",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "In 1975, a series of unexplained livestock deaths in the municipality of Moca, Puerto Rico, came to be attributed to a mysterious entity dubbed el vampiro de Moca ('the vampire of Moca').",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 1975, serangkaian kematian ternak tak terjelaskan di Moca, Puerto Riko, dikaitkan dengan sosok misterius yang dijuluki el vampiro de Moca (\"vampir dari Moca\").",
        "en": "In 1975, a series of unexplained livestock deaths in Moca, Puerto Rico, came to be attributed to a mysterious entity dubbed el vampiro de Moca (\"the vampire of Moca\")."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c11",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "The name is generally attributed to Puerto Rican comedian Silverio Pérez, who coined the term in 1995 while discussing the reported livestock attacks during his radio program in San Juan.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama itu umumnya dikaitkan dengan komedian Puerto Riko Silverio Pérez, yang mencetuskan istilah itu pada 1995 saat membahas serangan ternak dalam programa radionya di San Juan.",
        "en": "The name is generally attributed to Puerto Rican comedian Silverio Pérez, who coined the term in 1995 while discussing the reported livestock attacks on his radio program in San Juan."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c12",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "Reports of alleged sightings of the creature proliferated in the mid-1990s in various locations.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Laporan penampakan makhluk ini merebak pada pertengahan 1990-an di berbagai lokasi.",
        "en": "Reports of alleged sightings of the creature proliferated in the mid-1990s in various locations."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c13",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "In April 2000, in the mining city of Calama in northern Chile, approximately one hundred farm animals were reportedly found drained of blood or mutilated under unusual circumstances.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada April 2000, di kota tambang Calama di Chile utara, sekitar seratus hewan ternak dilaporkan ditemukan kehabisan darah atau termutilasi dalam keadaan tak lazim.",
        "en": "In April 2000, in the mining city of Calama in northern Chile, approximately one hundred farm animals were reportedly found drained of blood or mutilated under unusual circumstances."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c14",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "During the first half of 2002, reports of mutilated cattle emerged from several areas of Argentina",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada paruh pertama 2002, laporan ternak termutilasi muncul dari beberapa wilayah di Argentina.",
        "en": "During the first half of 2002, reports of mutilated cattle emerged from several areas of Argentina."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c15",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "In July 2004, a rancher killed a creature resembling a hairless dog, which he discovered attacking his livestock near San Antonio, Texas.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada Juli 2004, seorang peternak membunuh makhluk mirip anjing tanpa bulu yang ia temukan menyerang ternaknya di dekat San Antonio, Texas.",
        "en": "In July 2004, a rancher killed a creature resembling a hairless dog that he found attacking his livestock near San Antonio, Texas."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c16",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "However, these reports remain anecdotal and have not been supported by verifiable evidence. Investigations of purported sightings in northern Mexico and the Southern United States have instead identified the animals involved as canids afflicted by severe mange.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Laporan-laporan itu tetap berupa cerita dan tidak didukung bukti yang dapat diverifikasi; penyelidikan atas penampakan di Meksiko utara dan Amerika Serikat bagian selatan justru mengidentifikasi hewannya sebagai canid yang menderita kudis parah.",
        "en": "These reports remain anecdotal and unsupported by verifiable evidence; investigations of purported sightings in northern Mexico and the Southern United States instead identified the animals as canids afflicted by severe mange."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c17",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "Both chupacabras and chupacabra are used throughout the Americas; the former is the original Spanish term, while the latter emerged later as a regularized singular form.",
      "locator": "Name",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Chupacabras dan chupacabra sama-sama dipakai di seluruh Amerika; yang pertama adalah istilah Spanyol asli, sedangkan yang kedua muncul belakangan sebagai bentuk tunggal yang diregulerkan.",
        "en": "Both chupacabras and chupacabra are used throughout the Americas; the former is the original Spanish term, while the latter emerged later as a regularized singular form."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c18",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "The first reported attack later associated with the chupacabras phenomenon occurred in Puerto Rico in March 1995. Eight sheep were found dead, each reportedly bearing three puncture wounds to the chest and alleged to have been completely drained of blood.",
      "locator": "History",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Serangan pertama yang kemudian dikaitkan dengan fenomena chupacabras terjadi di Puerto Riko pada Maret 1995: delapan domba ditemukan mati, masing-masing konon dengan tiga luka tusuk di dada dan diklaim kehabisan darah sepenuhnya.",
        "en": "The first reported attack later associated with the chupacabras phenomenon occurred in Puerto Rico in March 1995: eight sheep were found dead, each reportedly with three puncture wounds to the chest and alleged to have been completely drained of blood."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c19",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "Several months later, in August, a resident named Madelyne Tolentino reported a sighting of the creature in the municipality of Canóvanas.",
      "locator": "History",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada Agustus, seorang warga bernama Madelyne Tolentino melaporkan penampakan makhluk itu di kotapraja Canóvanas.",
        "en": "In August, a resident named Madelyne Tolentino reported a sighting of the creature in the municipality of Canóvanas."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c20",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "A five-year investigation by Benjamin Radford, documented in his 2011 book Tracking the Chupacabra, concluded that the description given by the original eyewitness in Puerto Rico, Madelyne Tolentino, was based on the creature Sil in the 1995 science-fiction horror film Species.",
      "locator": "Reputed origin",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penyelidikan lima tahun Benjamin Radford, yang dibukukan dalam Tracking the Chupacabra (2011), menyimpulkan bahwa deskripsi saksi asli di Puerto Riko, Madelyne Tolentino, didasarkan pada makhluk Sil dalam film fiksi ilmiah horor Species (1995).",
        "en": "A five-year investigation by Benjamin Radford, documented in his 2011 book Tracking the Chupacabra, concluded that the description given by the original Puerto Rican eyewitness, Madelyne Tolentino, was based on the creature Sil in the 1995 science-fiction horror film Species."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c21",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "Dr. David Morales, a Puerto Rican veterinarian with the Department of Agriculture, analyzed 300 reported victims of the chupacabra and found that they had not been bled dry.",
      "locator": "Reputed origin",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dr. David Morales, dokter hewan Puerto Riko di Departemen Pertanian, menganalisis 300 korban yang dilaporkan dan menemukan bahwa mereka tidak kehabisan darah.",
        "en": "Dr. David Morales, a Puerto Rican veterinarian with the Department of Agriculture, analyzed 300 reported victims of the chupacabra and found they had not been bled dry."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c22",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "In 2010, University of Michigan biologist Barry O'Connor concluded that all the chupacabra reports in the United States were simply coyotes infected with the parasite Sarcoptes scabiei",
      "locator": "Reputed origin",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 2010, biolog Universitas Michigan Barry O'Connor menyimpulkan bahwa semua laporan chupacabra di Amerika Serikat sekadar koyote yang terinfeksi parasit Sarcoptes scabiei.",
        "en": "In 2010, University of Michigan biologist Barry O'Connor concluded that all chupacabra reports in the United States were simply coyotes infected with the parasite Sarcoptes scabiei."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c23",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "The most common description of the chupacabra is that of a reptile-like creature with leathery or scaly greenish-gray skin and a row of sharp spines or quills extending along its back. It is described as approximately 3 to 4 feet (0.9 to 1.2 m) tall and capable of standing and hopping in a manner resembling a kangaroo.",
      "locator": "Appearance",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Deskripsi yang paling umum adalah makhluk mirip reptil berkulit abu-abu kehijauan yang berkulit liat atau bersisik dengan deretan duri atau bulu tajam di punggung, setinggi sekitar 3 hingga 4 kaki, dan mampu berdiri serta melompat seperti kanguru.",
        "en": "The most common description is a reptile-like creature with leathery or scaly greenish-gray skin and a row of sharp spines or quills along its back, about 3 to 4 feet tall and able to stand and hop like a kangaroo."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c24",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "Another common portrayal of the chupacabra is as an unusual breed of wild dog. This form is typically described as mostly hairless with a pronounced spinal ridge, prominent eye sockets, fangs, and claws.",
      "locator": "Appearance",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penggambaran umum lain adalah anjing liar yang tak lazim, umumnya digambarkan hampir tanpa bulu dengan punggung berpunuk, rongga mata menonjol, taring, dan cakar.",
        "en": "Another common portrayal is of an unusual breed of wild dog, typically described as mostly hairless with a pronounced spinal ridge, prominent eye sockets, fangs and claws."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c25",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "Accounts of this type began emerging in the early 2000s, initially from areas extending northward from the Yucatán Peninsula and northern Mexico, before spreading into the United States. It subsequently become the predominant depiction of the chupacabra.",
      "locator": "Appearance",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Laporan jenis anjing ini mulai muncul pada awal 2000-an, semula dari kawasan di utara Semenanjung Yucatán dan Meksiko utara sebelum menyebar ke Amerika Serikat, dan kemudian menjadi penggambaran yang dominan.",
        "en": "Accounts of this dog type began emerging in the early 2000s, initially from areas north of the Yucatán Peninsula and northern Mexico before spreading to the United States, and it subsequently became the predominant depiction."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c26",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "Unlike conventional predators, the creature is said to drain the blood, and sometimes consume the organs of its victims, typically through three puncture wounds arranged in a downwards-pointing triangle, although some accounts describe only one or two wounds.",
      "locator": "Appearance",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tidak seperti pemangsa biasa, makhluk ini dikatakan menguras darah dan kadang memakan organ korban, biasanya lewat tiga luka tusuk berbentuk segitiga menunjuk ke bawah, walau sebagian laporan hanya menyebut satu atau dua luka.",
        "en": "Unlike conventional predators, the creature is said to drain blood and sometimes eat the organs of its victims, typically through three puncture wounds in a downward-pointing triangle, although some accounts describe only one or two wounds."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c27",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "After the story gained national attention, experts from Texas State University-San Marcos offered to conduct DNA testing. The analysis identified the creature as a coyote.",
      "locator": "History",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Setelah kisahnya menarik perhatian nasional, pakar Texas State University-San Marcos menawarkan uji DNA, dan analisis itu mengidentifikasi makhluk tersebut sebagai koyote.",
        "en": "After the story gained national attention, experts from Texas State University-San Marcos offered DNA testing, and the analysis identified the creature as a coyote."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c28",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "Unsatisfied, Canion contacted experts at the University of California, Davis for a second analysis, which determined that the creature was specifically a hybrid: a cross between a coyote and a Mexican wolf.",
      "locator": "History",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Karena tidak puas, Canion menghubungi pakar University of California, Davis untuk analisis kedua, yang menetapkan bahwa makhluk itu hibrida koyote dan serigala Meksiko.",
        "en": "Unsatisfied, Canion contacted experts at the University of California, Davis for a second analysis, which determined that the creature was a hybrid: a cross between a coyote and a Mexican wolf."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c29",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "In the Philippines, another legendary creature called the sigbin shares many of the features",
      "locator": "Related legends",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Filipina, makhluk legendaris lain bernama sigbin memiliki banyak ciri yang mirip dengan deskripsi chupacabra.",
        "en": "In the Philippines, another legendary creature called the sigbin shares many of the features in descriptions of the chupacabra."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c30",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s1",
      "quote": "The Peuchens of Chile also share similarities in their supposed habits, but instead of being dog-like they are described as winged snakes.",
      "locator": "Related legends",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Peuchen dari Chile juga memiliki kemiripan dalam kebiasaan yang dikatakan, tetapi digambarkan sebagai ular bersayap, bukan mirip anjing.",
        "en": "The Peuchens of Chile also share similarities in their supposed habits, but instead of being dog-like they are described as winged snakes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c31",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s2",
      "quote": "Stories of El Chupacabra first surfaced in March 1995 in Puerto Rico, Radford said, when dead, blood-drained goats began showing up (El Chupacabra translates to \"goat sucker\").",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kisah El Chupacabra pertama muncul pada Maret 1995 di Puerto Riko, ketika kambing-kambing mati yang kehabisan darah mulai ditemukan (El Chupacabra berarti \"penghisap kambing\").",
        "en": "Stories of El Chupacabra first surfaced in March 1995 in Puerto Rico, when dead, blood-drained goats began showing up (El Chupacabra translates to \"goat sucker\")."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c32",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s2",
      "quote": "That August, a newspaper printed an eyewitness description of a bipedal creature, 4 to 5 feet tall with spikes down its back, long, thin arms and legs, and an alienlike oblong head with red or black eyes.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada Agustus itu, sebuah surat kabar memuat deskripsi saksi mata tentang makhluk berkaki dua setinggi 4 sampai 5 kaki dengan duri di punggung, lengan dan kaki panjang kurus, serta kepala lonjong mirip makhluk asing dengan mata merah atau hitam.",
        "en": "That August a newspaper printed an eyewitness description of a bipedal creature 4 to 5 feet tall with spikes down its back, long thin arms and legs, and an alien-like oblong head with red or black eyes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c33",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s2",
      "quote": "The frenzy had died down slightly by 2000, but picked back up in 2004 when something began attacking livestock in Texas. ... They looked nothing like the Puerto Rico original, though, and DNA tests later revealed that they were actually coyotes with a severe case of mange.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bangkai-bangkai di Texas tidak mirip sosok asli Puerto Riko, dan uji DNA kemudian menunjukkan bahwa mereka sebenarnya koyote yang menderita kudis parah.",

    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c34",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s2",
      "quote": "\"When you did a necropsy of the chickens and goats that they attacked, they all had normal blood levels,\" Radford told Life's Little Mysteries. \"They were not, in fact, vampirized.\"",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {

        "en": "Radford said that a necropsy of the chickens and goats that were attacked showed normal blood levels; they were not, in fact, \"vampirized\"."

      "id": "chupacabra-c35",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s2",
      "quote": "Radford dug through every El Chupacabra mention and traced the physical description of the monster to a single event in the second week of August 1995, when a sketch from an eyewitness named Madelyne Tolentino ran in a Puerto Rican newspaper.",

El Chupacabra was one of the first mythical beasts discovered in the Internet age, and its image and story spread around the world - and especially to Spanish-speaking countries - in a matter of weeks.",

      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Radford, El Chupacabra adalah salah satu makhluk mitos pertama yang ditemukan pada era internet, dan gambaran serta kisahnya menyebar ke seluruh dunia, terutama ke negara berbahasa Spanyol, dalam hitungan pekan.",
        "en": "According to Radford, El Chupacabra was one of the first mythical beasts discovered in the Internet age, and its image and story spread around the world, especially to Spanish-speaking countries, in a matter of weeks."

    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c37",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s2",
      "quote": "\"By the mid-2000s, anything weird was being called El Chupacabra,\" he said. \"Mangy coyotes. Dead raccoons. Even a dried fish in New Mexico, which looks nothing like El Chupacabra.\"",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Radford mengatakan bahwa pada pertengahan 2000-an apa pun yang aneh disebut El Chupacabra: koyote berkudis, rakun mati, bahkan ikan kering di New Mexico.",
        "en": "Radford said that by the mid-2000s anything weird was being called El Chupacabra: mangy coyotes, dead raccoons, even a dried fish in New Mexico."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c38",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s3",
      "quote": "In the summer of 1995, a wave of livestock killings rocked the towns of rural Puerto Rico. The victims were found with wounds at their throats, seemingly empty of blood.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada musim panas 1995, gelombang pembunuhan ternak mengguncang kota-kota pedesaan Puerto Riko; para korban ditemukan dengan luka di leher dan tampak tanpa darah.",
        "en": "In the summer of 1995 a wave of livestock killings rocked the towns of rural Puerto Rico; the victims were found with wounds at their throats, seemingly empty of blood."

    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c39",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s3",

      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam 21 tahun sejak dugaan penampakan pertama, makhluk ini pernah menjadi alien berpunggung duri, kanguru bersayap atau goblin, monyet pemangsa, atau musang yang luar biasa ambisius.",

      "quote": "the chupacabra did not exist prior to 1995; it has no folkloric predecessors to fall back on.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {

        "en": "The chupacabra did not exist before 1995 and has no folkloric predecessors to fall back on."

      "id": "chupacabra-c41",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s3",
      "quote": "In the wake of Tolentino's account, aided by a sketch she later produced with local UFO researcher Jorge Martin, reports of bipedal, spiny and red-eyed creatures proliferated - over 200 in Puerto Rico alone.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Setelah laporan Tolentino, laporan makhluk berkaki dua, berduri, dan bermata merah membanjir, lebih dari 200 di Puerto Riko saja.",
        "en": "After Tolentino's account, reports of bipedal, spiny and red-eyed creatures proliferated, over 200 in Puerto Rico alone."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c42",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s3",
      "quote": "In 2000, Radford said, a Nicaraguan rancher shot and wounded something attacking his goats. A few days later a ranch hand found the carcass - a hairless, rangy-looking canid.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 2000, kata Radford, seorang peternak Nikaragua menembak dan melukai sesuatu yang menyerang kambingnya; beberapa hari kemudian bangkainya ditemukan: canid kurus tanpa bulu.",
        "en": "In 2000, Radford said, a Nicaraguan rancher shot and wounded something attacking his goats; a few days later its carcass was found, a hairless, rangy-looking canid."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c43",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s3",
      "quote": "In 2004, 2007 and 2009, several chupacabra carcasses popped up in the central part of the state, many with warty skin and protruding teeth. All of them, including Canion's, have been subjected to DNA testing, and the results have been the same: mangy, sickly coyotes or dogs.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 2004, 2007, dan 2009, beberapa bangkai \"chupacabra\" muncul di bagian tengah negara bagian itu; semuanya diuji DNA dengan hasil sama: koyote atau anjing yang berkudis dan sakit.",
        "en": "In 2004, 2007 and 2009 several chupacabra carcasses turned up in the central part of the state; all were DNA-tested with the same result: mangy, sickly coyotes or dogs."
      }
    },

      "id": "chupacabra-c44",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s3",
      "quote": "But domestic dogs often kill with a bite to the neck and then leave the carcass. By the time the kill is found, its blood has settled to the bottom - with a rip or puncture at the neck the only sign of attack.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Anjing peliharaan sering membunuh dengan gigitan di leher lalu meninggalkan bangkai; saat ditemukan, darahnya sudah mengendap di bagian bawah, dan robekan atau tusukan di leher menjadi satu-satunya tanda serangan.",
        "en": "Domestic dogs often kill with a bite to the neck and then leave the carcass; by the time it is found the blood has settled to the bottom, with a rip or puncture at the neck the only sign of attack."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chupacabra-c45",
      "source_id": "chupacabra-s3",
      "quote": "On July 7, 1995 - a month before the Tolentino sighting that sent the chupacabra rocketing to stardom - the sci-fi thriller Species was released across the United States and Puerto Rico.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 7 Juli 1995, sebulan sebelum penampakan Tolentino, film fiksi ilmiah Species dirilis di Amerika Serikat dan Puerto Riko.",
        "en": "On July 7, 1995, a month before the Tolentino sighting, the sci-fi thriller Species was released across the United States and Puerto Rico."

      "source_id": "chupacabra-s3",

      }

    "en": "Kurma is the tortoise form and second avatar of Vishnu, the Hindu preserver deity. He is best known from the legend of the churning of the Ocean of Milk, when he bore Mount Mandara on his back.",

      "id": "Kurma (dari kata Sanskerta untuk kura-kura) adalah avatar kedua Wisnu, dewa pemelihara Hindu, dan tercantum sebagai avatar kedua dalam Dashavatara, sepuluh inkarnasi utama Wisnu. Ia dikenal juga sebagai Akupara, kura-kura dunia yang menopang bumi. Dalam penuturan Hindu, ia muncul pada Satya Yuga sebagai kura-kura raksasa untuk menyelamatkan bumi dari kehancuran. Wisnu memiliki sepuluh avatar utama, dan Kurma disebut sebagai kura-kura bersama Matsya (ikan) dan Varaha (babi hutan).",
      "en": "Kurma (from the Sanskrit word for tortoise) is the second avatar of Vishnu, the Hindu preserver deity, and is listed as the second of the Dashavatara, the ten principal incarnations of Vishnu. He is also identified with Akupara, the World-Turtle who supports the Earth. The Hindu account has him arrive in the Satya Yuga as an immense turtle to rescue the earth from ruin. Hinduism also counts ten principal avatars, with Kurma named as the tortoise alongside Matsya the fish and Varaha the boar.",

Kurma disamakan dengan resi Kashyapa, yang namanya juga berarti kura-kura. Meski tidak ditemukan dalam Rigveda, Kashyapa muncul dalam himne-himnenya, dan Vajasaneyi Samhita menyebut kura-kura sebagai penguasa perairan.",
      "en": "Kurma's roots are traced to Vedic literature. The Shatapatha Brahmana is the earliest extant text to name him, and there Prajapati assumes the form of Kurma to create all creatures. Setelah nektar tercipta dan para iblis mencoba merebutnya, Wisnu menjelma sebagai perempuan Maya (Mohini) sehingga para dewa memperoleh keabadian. Menurut Bhagavata Purana, Wisnu juga dikatakan memberi semua dewa lain karunia keabadian.       "en": "Later literature such as the Puranas ties Kurma tightly to the Samudra Manthana, the story of stirring the Ocean of Milk. After the nectar is made and the demons try to seize it, Vishnu appears as the woman Maya (Mohini) so that the gods gain immortality.   "cultural_context": {
    "id": "Meskipun tidak banyak dipuja dalam Hindu modern, Kurma tetap diakui sebagai tokoh penting dalam kosmologi Hindu, dan karena Kurma-lah Wisnu bertemu Lakshmi.",
    "en": "Although not widely worshipped in modern Hinduism, Kurma is still recognised as an important figure in Hindu cosmology, and it was because of Kurma that Vishnu met Lakshmi.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "kurma-c45",
      "kurma-c46"
    ]
  },
  "etymology": {
    "original_form": "kūrma",
    "language": "Sanskrit",
    "literal_meaning": {
      "id": "'kura-kura' atau 'penyu'; menurut Shatapatha Brahmana, Prajapati disebut Kurma karena ia 'membuat' (kar) segalanya",
      "en": "'tortoise' or 'turtle'; according to the Shatapatha Brahmana, Prajapati was called Kurma since he 'made' (kar) all"
    },
    "claim_ids": [

      "kurma-c16",
      "kurma-c26"
    ]
  },
  "story_mode": {

  "stories": [
    {
      "title": {
        "id": "Pengadukan Samudra Susu (Samudra Manthana)",
        "en": "The churning of the Ocean of Milk (Samudra Manthana)"
      },
      "role": {
        "id": "Penopang Gunung Mandara",
        "en": "Supporter of Mount Mandara"
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Para dewa dan asura mengaduk samudra dengan Gunung Mandara dan ular Vasuki; ketika gunung itu tenggelam, Wisnu menopangnya sebagai Kurma, dan nektar keabadian akhirnya jatuh ke tangan para dewa.",
        "en": "The gods and asuras churn the ocean with Mount Mandara and the serpent Vasuki; when the mountain sinks, Vishnu supports it as Kurma, and the nectar of immortality ends up with the gods."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "kurma-c13",
        "kurma-c35",
        "kurma-c36",
        "kurma-c41",
        "kurma-c37",
        "kurma-c39"

    {

        "en": "The curse of Durvasa"
      },
      "role": {

        "en": "The event preceding Kurma's appearance"
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Durvasa mengutuk Indra dan para dewa agar kehilangan kekuatan setelah kalung bunganya diinjak gajah Airavata.",
        "en": "Durvasa cursed Indra and the devas to lose their strength after his garland was trampled by the elephant Airavata."
      },
      "claim_ids": [

    },
    {

        "en": "King of tortoises supporting the mountain"
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Para dewa memilih Gunung Mandara dan Vasuki-Ananta, lalu meminta Kurma-raja menopang gunung di punggungnya; narasi ini tidak mengaitkan Kurma dengan Wisnu.",
        "en": "The gods choose Mount Mandara and Vasuki-Ananta and then ask Kurma-raja to support the mount on his back; this narrative does not link Kurma with Vishnu."
      },
      "claim_ids": [

      "note": {
        "id": "Avatar Wisnu sebelum Kurma dalam Dashavatara; Kurma, sang amfibi, dipandang sebagai tahap berikutnya setelah ikan, dan asal-usul Matsya juga tercatat dalam Shatapatha Brahmana.",
        "en": "The avatar before Kurma in the Dashavatara; Kurma, the amphibian, is seen as the next stage after the fish, and the Shatapatha Brahmana also records the origins of Matsya."

      "id": "Mengapa Kurma dilukiskan sebagai penopang, bukan pelaku utama, dalam pengadukan samudra?",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "kurma-c01",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "is the second avatar of the Hindu preserver deity, Vishnu.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kurma adalah avatar kedua dari dewa pemelihara Hindu, Wisnu.",
        "en": "Kurma is the second avatar of the Hindu preserver deity, Vishnu."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c02",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "Originating in Vedic literature such as the Yajurveda as being synonymous with the Saptarishi called Kashyapa, Kurma is most commonly associated in post-Vedic literature such as the Puranas. He prominently appears in the legend of the churning of the Ocean of Milk, referred to as the Samudra Manthana.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kurma berakar dari literatur Weda dan dikaitkan erat dengan legenda Samudra Manthana.",
        "en": "Kurma originates in Vedic texts and is associated with the Samudra Manthana."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c03",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "He prominently appears in the legend of the churning of the Ocean of Milk, referred to as the Samudra Manthana.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia tampil menonjol dalam legenda pengadukan lautan susu Samudra Manthana.",
        "en": "He prominently appears in the legend of the churning of the Ocean of Milk."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c04",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "Along with being synonymous with Akupara, the World-Turtle supporting the Earth, Kurma is listed as the 2nd avatar in the Dashavatara, which are the ten principal incarnations of Vishnu.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kurma disinonimkan dengan Akupara, kura-kura penopang bumi, sebagai awatara Wisnu.",
        "en": "Kurma is synonymous with Akupara, the World-Turtle supporting the Earth."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c05",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "The Sanskrit word 'Kurma' (Devanagari: कूर्म) means 'Tortoise' and 'Turtle'.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kata Sanskerta Kurma secara harfiah bermakna kura-kura atau penyu.",
        "en": "The Sanskrit word Kurma literally means tortoise and turtle."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c06",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "The tortoise incarnation of Vishnu is also referred to in post-Vedic literature such as the Bhagavata Purana as 'Kacchapa' (कच्छप), 'Kamaṭha' (कमठ), 'Akupara' (अकूपार), and 'Ambucara-Atmana' (अम्बुचर-आत्मना), all of which mean 'tortoise' or 'form of a tortoise'.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Inkarnasi kura-kura Wisnu juga disebut Kacchapa, Kamaṭha, Akupara, dan Ambucara-Atmana dalam sastra pasca-Weda seperti Bhagavata Purana; semuanya berarti \"kura-kura\" atau \"wujud kura-kura\".",
        "en": "The tortoise incarnation of Vishnu is also called Kacchapa, Kamaṭha, Akupara and Ambucara-Atmana in post-Vedic literature such as the Bhagavata Purana, all meaning \"tortoise\" or \"form of a tortoise\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c07",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "Kurmasana (Tortoise Posture) is a Yoga posture.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kurmasana adalah postur kura-kura dalam disiplin yoga.",
        "en": "Kurmasana (Tortoise Posture) is a Yoga posture."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c08",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "The Dashavataras are compared to evolution; Kurma - the amphibian - is regarded the next stage after Matysa, the fish.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Dashavataras, Kurma melambangkan tahap evolusi amfibi setelah ikan Matsya.",
        "en": "In the Dashavataras, Kurma represents the amphibian stage following Matsya."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c09",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "The Shatapatha Brahmana is the earliest extant text to mention Kurma, the tortoise.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kitab Shatapatha Brahmana adalah teks tertua yang mencatat Kurma sang kura-kura.",
        "en": "The Shatapatha Brahmana is the earliest extant text to mention Kurma."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c10",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "The Shatapatha Brahmana equates the tortoise - Kurma to the creator of all creatures. The god Prajapati assumes the form of Kurma to create all creatures (praja).",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kitab Shatapatha Brahmana menyamakan Kurma dengan dewa pencipta Prajapati.",
        "en": "The Shatapatha Brahmana equates Kurma with the creator deity Prajapati."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c11",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "The Shatapatha Brahmana also has the origins of Matsya, the Fish.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kitab Shatapatha Brahmana juga memuat asal-usul awatara ikan Matsya.",
        "en": "The Shatapatha Brahmana also records the origins of Matsya the Fish."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c12",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "Though Kurma is not found in the oldest Hindu scripture Rigveda, the seer Kashyapa (who is equated with Kurma) appears in hymns in the scripture.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Meski nama Kurma tidak ada di Rigveda, resi Kashyapa yang disamakan dengan Kurma disebut dalam himne.",
        "en": "Though Kurma is absent in the Rigveda, seer Kashyapa appears in hymns."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c13",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "In post-Vedic literature, including the Puranas, Kurma is inextricably linked with the legend of the churning of the Ocean Of Milk, known as the Samudra Manthana.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam sastra pasca-Weda, termasuk Purana, Kurma terkait erat dengan legenda Samudra Manthana (pengadukan Lautan Susu).",
        "en": "In post-Vedic literature, including the Puranas, Kurma is closely tied to the Samudra Manthana legend."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c14",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "There are five temples dedicated to this incarnation of Vishnu in India: Kurmai (Chittoor District of Andhra Pradesh) Sri Kurmam (Srikakulam District of Andhra Pradesh)",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ada lima kuil yang dipersembahkan bagi inkarnasi Wisnu ini di India, termasuk Kurmai (Distrik Chittoor, Andhra Pradesh) dan Sri Kurmam (Distrik Srikakulam, Andhra Pradesh).",
        "en": "There are five temples dedicated to this incarnation of Vishnu in India, including Kurmai (Chittoor District, Andhra Pradesh) and Sri Kurmam (Srikakulam District, Andhra Pradesh)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c15",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "Gavirangapura (Chitradurga District of Karnataka) Swarupnarayan (Goghat village in Hooghly district of West Bengal).",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kuil lain bagi inkarnasi ini mencakup Gavirangapura (Distrik Chitradurga, Karnataka) dan Swarupnarayan (desa Goghat, distrik Hooghly, Benggala Barat).",
        "en": "Other temples for this incarnation include Gavirangapura (Chitradurga District, Karnataka) and Swarupnarayan (Goghat village, Hooghly district, West Bengal)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c16",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "The god Prajapati assumes the form of Kurma to create all creatures (praja). Since he \"made\" (kar) all, Prajapati's form was called Kurma.",
      "locator": "Vedas",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dewa Prajapati mengambil wujud Kurma untuk menciptakan semua makhluk (praja); karena ia \"membuat\" (kar) segalanya, wujud Prajapati disebut Kurma.",
        "en": "The god Prajapati assumes the form of Kurma to create all creatures (praja); since he \"made\" (kar) all, Prajapati's form was called Kurma."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c17",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "Kurma is equated with Kashyapa (literally \"tortoise\"), thus all creatures are called \"children of Kashyapa\". Kurma is also called Surya (the sun).",
      "locator": "Vedas",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kurma disamakan dengan Kashyapa (secara harfiah \"kura-kura\"), sehingga semua makhluk disebut \"anak Kashyapa\"; Kurma juga disebut Surya (matahari).",
        "en": "Kurma is equated with Kashyapa (literally \"tortoise\"), so all creatures are called \"children of Kashyapa\"; Kurma is also called Surya (the sun)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c18",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "The Taittiriya Samhita suggests a ritual of burying a live tortoise at the base of the sacrificial fire altar (uttar-vedi). By this act, the sacrificer earns the merit of reaching heaven.",
      "locator": "Vedas",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Taittiriya Samhita menyarankan ritual mengubur kura-kura hidup di dasar altar api kurban; dengan itu pemberi kurban memperoleh pahala mencapai surga.",
        "en": "The Taittiriya Samhita suggests a ritual of burying a live tortoise at the base of the sacrificial fire altar; by this act the sacrificer earns the merit of reaching heaven."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c19",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "the Vajasaneyi Samhita of the white Yajurveda describes the tortoise as the \"lord of the waters\".",
      "locator": "Vedas",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Vajasaneyi Samhita dari Yajurveda putih menggambarkan kura-kura sebagai \"penguasa perairan\".",
        "en": "The Vajasaneyi Samhita of the white Yajurveda describes the tortoise as the \"lord of the waters\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c20",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "In the Adi Parva Book of the epic Mahabharata, the god Narayana (identified with Vishnu) suggests the gods (devas) and the demons (asuras) churn the ocean to obtain amrita (ambrosia) as both of them seek immortality. The gods select Mount Mandara as the churning rod and the serpent Vasuki-Ananta as the rope.",
      "locator": "Epics",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam kitab Adi Parva epos Mahabharata, dewa Narayana (diidentifikasi dengan Wisnu) menyarankan para dewa dan asura mengaduk samudra untuk memperoleh amrita karena keduanya mendambakan keabadian; para dewa memilih Gunung Mandara sebagai tongkat pengaduk dan ular Vasuki-Ananta sebagai tali.",
        "en": "In the Adi Parva book of the Mahabharata, the god Narayana (identified with Vishnu) suggests that the devas and asuras churn the ocean to obtain amrita because both seek immortality; the gods choose Mount Mandara as the churning rod and the serpent Vasuki-Ananta as the rope."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c21",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "Then they approach Kurma-raja, the king of tortoises to support the mount on its back, which it consented.",
      "locator": "Epics",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka lalu mendekati Kurma-raja, raja para kura-kura, agar menopang gunung itu di punggungnya, dan ia menyetujui.",
        "en": "They then approach Kurma-raja, the king of tortoises, to support the mount on its back, and it consents."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c22",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "In this narrative, Kurma is not related with Vishnu. Though the critical edition of the epic does not refer to Kurma as an avatar of Vishnu, some latter insertions in manuscripts of the epic associate Kurma as a pradurbhava (manifestation) of Vishnu.",
      "locator": "Epics",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam narasi ini Kurma tidak dikaitkan dengan Wisnu; edisi kritis epos itu tidak menyebut Kurma sebagai avatar Wisnu, meski beberapa sisipan belakangan pada naskah mengaitkannya sebagai pradurbhava (perwujudan) Wisnu.",
        "en": "In this narrative Kurma is not related to Vishnu; the critical edition of the epic does not call Kurma an avatar of Vishnu, though some later insertions in manuscripts associate Kurma as a pradurbhava (manifestation) of Vishnu."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c23",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "The Kurma Purana is one of four Puranas that bear the names of Vishnu's avatars.",
      "locator": "Puranas",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kurma Purana adalah salah satu dari empat Purana yang memakai nama avatar Wisnu.",
        "en": "The Kurma Purana is one of four Puranas that bear the names of Vishnu's avatars."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c24",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "Meditation / Churning the Mind: Aiyangar also surmises that the legend of the Samudra Manthana symbolises churning the mind through Meditation to achieve liberation (Moksha).",
      "locator": "Symbolism",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Aiyangar menduga legenda Samudra Manthana melambangkan pengadukan pikiran lewat meditasi untuk mencapai pembebasan (moksha).",
        "en": "Aiyangar surmises that the legend of the Samudra Manthana symbolises churning the mind through meditation to achieve liberation (moksha)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c25",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "Kashyapa - also meaning 'Tortoise' - is considered the Progenitor of all living beings with his thirteen wives, including vegetation",
      "locator": "Kashyapa",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kashyapa, yang juga berarti \"kura-kura\", dianggap leluhur semua makhluk hidup bersama tiga belas istrinya, termasuk tumbuh-tumbuhan.",
        "en": "Kashyapa, also meaning 'tortoise', is considered the progenitor of all living beings with his thirteen wives, including vegetation."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c26",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "A tortoise is also called a-kupa-ara, because it does not move in a well [On account of its shallowness].",
      "locator": "The Nirukta",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kura-kura juga disebut a-kupa-ara karena tidak bergerak dalam sumur (karena dangkalnya).",
        "en": "A tortoise is also called a-kupa-ara because it does not move in a well (on account of its shallowness)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c27",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "meaning 'Hand Tortoise', is a special positioning of the fingers during worship rituals to symbolise Kurma.",
      "locator": "Yoga",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "'Panikacchapika', yang berarti 'kura-kura tangan', adalah posisi jari khusus dalam ritual pemujaan untuk melambangkan Kurma.",
        "en": "'Panikacchapika', meaning 'Hand Tortoise', is a special positioning of the fingers during worship rituals to symbolise Kurma."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c28",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "Affiliation Vaishnavism Abode Bharata Khanda, Vaikuntha, Srikurmam Mantra Om Kurmaya Namah Weapon None Festivals Kurma Jayanti Consort Lakshmi as Sindhusuta",
      "locator": "Infobox",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kotak info Wikipedia mencantumkan afiliasi Waisnawa, tempat tinggal di Bharata Khanda, Vaikuntha, dan Srikurmam, mantra Om Kurmaya Namah, tanpa senjata, perayaan Kurma Jayanti, dan pasangan Lakshmi sebagai Sindhusuta.",
        "en": "The article infobox lists Vaishnavism as affiliation, abode in Bharata Khanda, Vaikuntha and Srikurmam, the mantra Om Kurmaya Namah, no weapon, the festival Kurma Jayanti, and Lakshmi as Sindhusuta for consort."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c29",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "Notable depictions include the relief at Angkor Wat with Vishnu and Kurma in the centre and the gods and demons on either side churning the ocean.",
      "locator": "Iconography",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penggambaran terkenal mencakup relief di Angkor Wat dengan Wisnu dan Kurma di tengah serta para dewa dan iblis di kedua sisi mengaduk samudra.",
        "en": "Notable depictions include the relief at Angkor Wat with Vishnu and Kurma in the centre and the gods and demons on either side churning the ocean."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c30",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "Srikurmam was initially a Shiva temple, which was converted into a Vaishnava one by the Vaishnava saint Ramanuja.",
      "locator": "Locations",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Srikurmam semula kuil Siwa yang diubah menjadi kuil Waisnawa oleh santo Waisnawa Ramanuja.",
        "en": "Srikurmam was initially a Shiva temple, which was converted into a Vaishnava one by the Vaishnava saint Ramanuja."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c31",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "Nanditha Krishna suggests that a tribal tortoise god could have been assimilated in the Hindu fold by identifying him with Kurma.",
      "locator": "Locations",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nanditha Krishna menduga bahwa dewa kura-kura suku dapat diserap ke dalam lingkungan Hindu dengan mengidentifikasikannya sebagai Kurma.",
        "en": "Nanditha Krishna suggests that a tribal tortoise god could have been assimilated into the Hindu fold by identifying him with Kurma."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c32",
      "source_id": "kurma-s2",
      "quote": "Vishnu has ten avatars or worldly appearances, which are people, animals or a mix of both.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Wisnu memiliki sepuluh avatar atau penampakan duniawi, yang berupa manusia, hewan, atau campuran keduanya.",
        "en": "Vishnu has ten avatars or worldly appearances, which are people, animals or a mix of both."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c33",
      "source_id": "kurma-s2",
      "quote": "Matsya (the fish) Kurma (the tortoise) Varaha (the boar)",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam daftar avatar, Kurma disebut sebagai kura-kura, bersama Matsya (ikan) dan Varaha (babi hutan).",
        "en": "In the list of avatars, Kurma is named as the tortoise, alongside Matsya (the fish) and Varaha (the boar)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c34",
      "source_id": "kurma-s2",
      "quote": "In the Bhagavata Purana, an epic poem that collects together many Vaishnavite stories, Vishnu is also credited with giving all the other gods the gift of immortality.",
      "locator": "Vishnu in Mythology",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Bhagavata Purana, puisi epik yang menghimpun banyak kisah Waisnawa, Wisnu juga dikatakan memberi semua dewa lain karunia keabadian.",
        "en": "In the Bhagavata Purana, an epic poem collecting many Vaishnavite stories, Vishnu is also credited with giving all the other gods the gift of immortality."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c35",
      "source_id": "kurma-s2",
      "quote": "To mix the ocean they decided to use the holy Mt. Mandara with the giant serpent Vasuki (or Ananta) as a turning rope; one end to be pulled by Demons and the other by the gods.",
      "locator": "Vishnu in Mythology",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Untuk mengaduk samudra mereka memakai Gunung Mandara yang suci dengan ular raksasa Vasuki (atau Ananta) sebagai tali; satu ujung ditarik para iblis dan ujung lain oleh para dewa.",
        "en": "To mix the ocean they used the holy Mt. Mandara with the giant serpent Vasuki (or Ananta) as a rope, one end pulled by the demons and the other by the gods."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c36",
      "source_id": "kurma-s2",
      "quote": "However, neither group could handle such a weight and they called for Vishnu to hold it. This he did in the form of Kurma, the giant tortoise, supporting the mountain on his shell.",
      "locator": "Vishnu in Mythology",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namun tak satu pun pihak sanggup menanggung beban itu sehingga mereka memanggil Wisnu untuk menahannya; ia melakukannya dalam wujud Kurma, kura-kura raksasa, menopang gunung di cangkangnya.",
        "en": "Neither group could handle such a weight, so they called for Vishnu to hold it; he did so in the form of Kurma, the giant tortoise, supporting the mountain on his shell."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c37",
      "source_id": "kurma-s2",
      "quote": "The nectar was duly created from the foaming sea but the Demons, true to character, tried to make off with it. Fortunately, Vishnu interceded in the form of the beautiful Maya (the personification of Illusion) and, suitably distracted, the Demons relinquished the nectar which Vishnu graciously gave to the gods, allowing them the possibility of immortality.",
      "locator": "Vishnu in Mythology",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nektar tercipta dari laut berbusa, tetapi para iblis mencoba membawanya kabur; Wisnu turun tangan dalam wujud Maya yang cantik dan, karena para iblis terkecoh, nektar itu diberikan kepada para dewa sehingga mereka berpeluang abadi.",
        "en": "The nectar was created from the foaming sea but the demons tried to make off with it; Vishnu intervened as the beautiful Maya and, with the demons distracted, gave the nectar to the gods, allowing them the possibility of immortality."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c38",
      "source_id": "kurma-s3",
      "quote": "In the Hindu religion, Kurma (Sanskrit: meaning \"Tortoise\") is the second avatar of Vishnu, the preserver god in the Hindu Trimurti (trinity), who appeared in the Satya Yuga as a giant turtle to save the earth from destruction.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam agama Hindu, Kurma (Sanskerta: berarti \"kura-kura\") adalah avatar kedua Wisnu, dewa pemelihara dalam Trimurti Hindu, yang muncul pada Satya Yuga sebagai kura-kura raksasa untuk menyelamatkan bumi dari kehancuran.",
        "en": "In Hinduism Kurma (Sanskrit: \"Tortoise\") is the second avatar of Vishnu, the preserver god in the Hindu Trimurti, who appeared in the Satya Yuga as a giant turtle to save the earth from destruction."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c39",
      "source_id": "kurma-s3",
      "quote": "His enormous back was said to have provided a foundation for the mythical Mount Mandara, which was used by the gods (and demons) as a churning rod to stir the primordial milk-ocean thereby obtaining the purported nectar of immortality.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Punggungnya yang besar dikatakan menjadi landasan bagi Gunung Mandara yang mitis, yang dipakai para dewa (dan iblis) sebagai tongkat pengaduk samudra susu purba untuk memperoleh nektar keabadian.",
        "en": "His enormous back was said to provide a foundation for the mythical Mount Mandara, used by the gods (and demons) as a churning rod to stir the primordial milk-ocean and obtain the nectar of immortality."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c40",
      "source_id": "kurma-s3",
      "quote": "It is said that Durvasa, an ancient sage, gave a garland of flowers to Indra. Indra placed this garland upon his elephant Airavata, who promptly threw it on the ground and trampled it. Having witnessed this desecration of his gift, the short-tempered Durvasa cursed Indra and all the other devas (benevolent gods) so that they would lose their strength.",
      "locator": "Mythology",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Konon resi kuno Durvasa memberi Indra kalung bunga; Indra menaruhnya pada gajahnya Airavata, yang melemparkan dan menginjaknya; Durvasa yang pemarah mengutuk Indra dan para dewa agar kehilangan kekuatan.",
        "en": "It is said that the ancient sage Durvasa gave Indra a garland of flowers; Indra placed it upon his elephant Airavata, who threw it down and trampled it; the short-tempered Durvasa cursed Indra and the devas so that they would lose their strength."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c41",
      "source_id": "kurma-s3",
      "quote": "As the churning continued, Mt. Mandara began to gradually sink into the mud at the bottom of the milk-ocean. In order to provide support for the mountain so it would not sink any further, Vishnu took the form of Kurma, the tortoise, and supported the mountain upon his broad back.",
      "locator": "Mythology",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seiring pengadukan, Gunung Mandara perlahan tenggelam ke lumpur dasar samudra susu; agar tidak tenggelam lebih dalam, Wisnu mengambil wujud Kurma, sang kura-kura, dan menopang gunung di punggungnya yang lebar.",
        "en": "As the churning continued Mt. Mandara began to sink into the mud at the bottom of the milk-ocean; so it would sink no further, Vishnu took the form of Kurma, the tortoise, and supported the mountain upon his broad back."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c42",
      "source_id": "kurma-s3",
      "quote": "The asuras promptly commandeered the elixir, however, thanks to another trick on the part of Vishnu in which he appeared as the female Mohini in order to dumbfound the asuras by way of seduction, the devas attained the elixir of immortality for themselves.",
      "locator": "Mythology",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para asura segera merebut ramuan itu, tetapi berkat tipuan Wisnu yang menjelma sebagai Mohini, para dewa memperoleh ramuan keabadian untuk diri mereka.",
        "en": "The asuras promptly commandeered the elixir, but thanks to another trick by Vishnu appearing as Mohini, the devas obtained the elixir of immortality for themselves."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c43",
      "source_id": "kurma-s3",
      "quote": "In Hindu iconography, Kurma is depicted as a tortoise, or else as a human being with a tortoise head or a tortoise body. His colour is black, although sometimes he is depicted as golden in colour.",
      "locator": "Depiction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam ikonografi Hindu, Kurma digambarkan sebagai kura-kura, atau sebagai manusia berkepala atau bertubuh kura-kura; warnanya hitam, meski kadang digambarkan keemasan.",
        "en": "In Hindu iconography Kurma is depicted as a tortoise, or as a human being with a tortoise head or body; his colour is black, although sometimes he is shown as golden."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c44",
      "source_id": "kurma-s3",
      "quote": "Kurma is commonly pictured with his consorts Sri and Bhumi, or else with Sri and the Pusti tree.",
      "locator": "Depiction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kurma lazim digambarkan bersama pasangannya Sri dan Bhumi, atau bersama Sri dan pohon Pusti.",
        "en": "Kurma is commonly pictured with his consorts Sri and Bhumi, or else with Sri and the Pusti tree."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c45",
      "source_id": "kurma-s3",
      "quote": "Although Kurma is not widely worshiped in modern Hinduism, he is still acknowledged as an important figure in Hindu cosmology.",
      "locator": "Significance",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Meskipun Kurma tidak banyak dipuja dalam Hindu modern, ia tetap diakui sebagai tokoh penting dalam kosmologi Hindu.",
        "en": "Although Kurma is not widely worshiped in modern Hinduism, he is still acknowledged as an important figure in Hindu cosmology."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c46",
      "source_id": "kurma-s3",
      "quote": "it is because of Kurma that Vishnu met his wife, the beloved Lakshmi",
      "locator": "Significance",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Karena Kurma-lah Wisnu bertemu istrinya, Lakshmi yang dicintai.",
        "en": "It is because of Kurma that Vishnu met his wife, the beloved Lakshmi."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c47",
      "source_id": "kurma-s3",
      "quote": "The story of the Kurma Avatar, found in the Kurma Purana, reflects the usurping of the early Hindu Vedic deities (such as Indra and Varuna) with the popular gods of Classical Hinduism such as the Hindu Trimurti.",
      "locator": "Mythology",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kisah Avatar Kurma, yang disebut terdapat dalam Kurma Purana, mencerminkan tergesernya dewa-dewa Weda awal (seperti Indra dan Varuna) oleh dewa-dewa populer Hindu Klasik seperti Trimurti.",
        "en": "The story of the Kurma Avatar, said to be found in the Kurma Purana, reflects the usurping of the early Vedic deities (such as Indra and Varuna) by the popular gods of Classical Hinduism such as the Trimurti."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c48",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "The detailed tale of the Samudra Manthana is absent from the Purana and alludes to Kurma as the one who supported Mount Mandara.",
      "locator": "Puranas",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kisah rinci Samudra Manthana tidak terdapat dalam Purana ini, yang hanya menyinggung Kurma sebagai penopang Gunung Mandara.",
        "en": "The detailed tale of the Samudra Manthana is absent from this Purana, which alludes to Kurma only as the one who supported Mount Mandara."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c49",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "The upper half resembles Vishnu and wears the traditional ornaments and the kirita-mukuta (tall conical crown) as worn by Vishnu.",
      "locator": "Iconography",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam bentuk antropomorfik, bagian atasnya menyerupai Wisnu dan mengenakan perhiasan tradisional serta kirita-mukuta, mahkota kerucut tinggi seperti yang dikenakan Wisnu.",
        "en": "In the anthropomorphic form the upper half resembles Vishnu and wears the traditional ornaments and the kirita-mukuta (tall conical crown) as worn by Vishnu."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurma-c50",
      "source_id": "kurma-s1",
      "quote": "In the anthropomorphic form, the upper half is that of the four-armed man and the lower half is a fish.",
      "locator": "Iconography",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam bentuk antropomorfik, bagian atas berupa pria berlengan empat dan bagian bawah berupa ikan.",
        "en": "In the anthropomorphic form, the upper half is that of the four-armed man and the lower half is a fish."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [

    {
      "topic": {

      },
      "positions": [
        {

            "en": "One source says the story of the Kurma Avatar is found in the Kurma Purana."
          },

            "kurma-c47"
