      "quote": "Mama: means mother, but also represents the feminine principle that generates and nourishes life.",
      "locator": "Etymology",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Unsur kedua, mama, berarti ibu, tetapi juga mewakili prinsip feminin yang melahirkan dan memelihara kehidupan.",
        "en": "The second part, mama, means mother, but also represents the feminine principle that generates and nourishes life."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pachamama-c14",
      "source_id": "pachamama-s1",
      "quote": "Pachamama plays the role of cosmic mother; from Pachamama's fertility, the cosmos is born, the earth is fertilized, and then it flourishes.",
      "locator": "Description of Pachamama",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pachamama berperan sebagai ibu kosmik; dari kesuburannya kosmos lahir, bumi disuburkan, dan kemudian berkembang.",
        "en": "Pachamama plays the role of cosmic mother; from her fertility the cosmos is born, the earth is fertilized, and it then flourishes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pachamama-c15",
      "source_id": "pachamama-s1",
      "quote": "Allpamama is often hungry, and if she is not nourished with offerings or if she is offended, she can cause a cosmic imbalance that breaks the universal harmony affecting all worlds",
      "locator": "Description of Pachamama",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Allpamama sering lapar, dan bila tidak diberi persembahan atau tersinggung, ia dapat menimbulkan ketidakseimbangan kosmik yang merusak keharmonisan semesta yang memengaruhi semua dunia.",
        "en": "Allpamama is often hungry, and if she is not nourished with offerings or is offended, she can cause a cosmic imbalance that breaks the universal harmony affecting all worlds."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pachamama-c16",
      "source_id": "pachamama-s1",
      "quote": "Pachamama and her sun-husband, Inti, are worshipped as benevolent deities in the area known as Tawantinsuyu. Tawantinsuyu is the name of the former Inca Empire, and the region stretches through the Andean mountains in present-day Bolivia, Ecuador, Chile, Peru, Colombia and northern Argentina.",
      "locator": "Modern-day rituals",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pachamama dan suaminya, sang matahari Inti, dipuja sebagai dewa-dewi yang murah hati di wilayah yang dikenal sebagai Tawantinsuyu, bekas Kekaisaran Inca yang membentang melintasi pegunungan Andes di Bolivia, Ekuador, Chile, Peru, Kolombia, dan Argentina utara masa kini.",
        "en": "Pachamama and her sun-husband Inti are worshipped as benevolent deities in the area known as Tawantinsuyu, the former Inca Empire, stretching through the Andean mountains in present-day Bolivia, Ecuador, Chile, Peru, Colombia and northern Argentina."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pachamama-c17",
      "source_id": "pachamama-s1",
      "quote": "In some regions, people perform a special kind of libation known as a challa on a daily basis. They spill a small amount of chicha on the floor, for the goddess, and then drink the rest.",
      "locator": "Modern-day rituals",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di beberapa daerah, orang melakukan persembahan cairan khusus bernama challa setiap hari: mereka menumpahkan sedikit chicha ke tanah untuk sang dewi, lalu meminum sisanya.",
        "en": "In some regions people perform a special libation known as a challa on a daily basis: they spill a little chicha on the floor for the goddess and then drink the rest."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pachamama-c18",
      "source_id": "pachamama-s1",
      "quote": "The central ritual to Pachamama is the Challa or Pago (payment). It is carried out during all of August, and in many places also on the first Friday of each month.",
      "locator": "Modern-day rituals",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ritual utama bagi Pachamama adalah Challa atau Pago (pembayaran), yang dilakukan sepanjang bulan Agustus dan di banyak tempat juga pada Jumat pertama setiap bulan.",
        "en": "The central ritual to Pachamama is the Challa or Pago (payment), carried out during all of August and in many places also on the first Friday of each month."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pachamama-c19",
      "source_id": "pachamama-s1",
      "quote": "Through religious syncretism, the figure of the Virgin Mary was associated with that of Pachamama for many Indigenous peoples.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Melalui sinkretisme agama, sosok Perawan Maria dikaitkan dengan Pachamama oleh banyak masyarakat adat.",
        "en": "Through religious syncretism, the figure of the Virgin Mary was associated with that of Pachamama for many Indigenous peoples."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pachamama-c20",
      "source_id": "pachamama-s1",
      "quote": "In the 21st century, many Indigenous peoples in South America base environmental concerns in these ancient beliefs, saying that problems arise when people take too much from nature because they are taking too much from Pachamama.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada abad ke-21, banyak masyarakat adat di Amerika Selatan mendasarkan kepedulian lingkungan pada kepercayaan kuno ini, dengan mengatakan bahwa masalah muncul ketika manusia mengambil terlalu banyak dari alam karena berarti mengambil terlalu banyak dari Pachamama.",
        "en": "In the 21st century, many Indigenous peoples in South America base environmental concerns on these ancient beliefs, saying that problems arise when people take too much from nature because they are taking too much from Pachamama."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pachamama-c21",
      "source_id": "pachamama-s1",
      "quote": "Parents Viracocha Consort Pacha Kamaq, Inti Offspring Inti Mama Killa",
      "locator": "Infobox",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kotak info Wikipedia mencantumkan Viracocha sebagai orang tua, Pacha Kamaq dan Inti sebagai pasangan, serta Inti dan Mama Killa sebagai keturunan Pachamama.",
        "en": "The article infobox lists Viracocha as parent, Pacha Kamaq and Inti as consorts, and Inti and Mama Killa as offspring of Pachamama."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pachamama-c22",
      "source_id": "pachamama-s2",
      "quote": "The Peru program at the 2015 Folklife Festival takes its name from the Pachamama, translating to \"Mother Earth\" in the native Quechua and Aymara languages",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Program Peru pada Festival Folklife 2015 memakai nama Pachamama, yang dalam bahasa asli Quechua dan Aymara berarti \"Ibu Bumi\".",
        "en": "The Peru program at the 2015 Folklife Festival takes its name from Pachamama, which means \"Mother Earth\" in the native Quechua and Aymara languages."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pachamama-c23",
      "source_id": "pachamama-s2",
      "quote": "In this story, Rufino Turpo (son of the late Nazario Turpo, guardian and Pacco ritual master of the sacred Ausangate Mountain) shares the life-sustaining role of Pachamama: mother of all living beings, the core of existence, the symbol of fertility.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Rufino Turpo menguraikan peran Pachamama yang menopang kehidupan: ibu semua makhluk hidup, inti keberadaan, dan lambang kesuburan.",
        "en": "Rufino Turpo describes the life-sustaining role of Pachamama: mother of all living beings, the core of existence, the symbol of fertility."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pachamama-c24",
      "source_id": "pachamama-s2",
      "quote": "On the first day of Chajrayapuy (August), our family gathers to make a \"payment\" or offering to the Pachamama. Like us, she needs to eat and drink. Whenever we drink chicha, beer, or wine, we need to share it with her.",
      "locator": "Account of the offering",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada hari pertama Chajrayapuy (Agustus), keluarga berkumpul untuk membuat \"pembayaran\" atau persembahan kepada Pachamama; seperti manusia, ia perlu makan dan minum, dan setiap kali minum chicha, bir, atau anggur, minuman itu harus dibagikan kepadanya.",
        "en": "On the first day of Chajrayapuy (August) the family gathers to make a \"payment\" or offering to Pachamama; like people, she needs to eat and drink, and whenever chicha, beer or wine is drunk it must be shared with her."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pachamama-c25",
      "source_id": "pachamama-s2",
      "quote": "We offer all this for Pachamama to eat, and in return she provides for us and protects us. Finally, we burn the offering. Smoke rises, taking our prayers.",
      "locator": "Account of the offering",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Semua persembahan itu diberikan agar Pachamama makan, dan sebagai balasannya ia menyediakan kebutuhan dan melindungi; persembahan lalu dibakar dan asapnya membawa doa.",
        "en": "All this is offered for Pachamama to eat, and in return she provides for the people and protects them; the offering is then burned and the smoke carries the prayers."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pachamama-c26",
      "source_id": "pachamama-s2",
      "quote": "The Mother gets angry when you want to pay without respect, without knowing why.",
      "locator": "Account of the offering",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sang Ibu marah bila orang membayar tanpa hormat dan tanpa tahu alasannya.",
        "en": "The Mother gets angry when you want to pay without respect, without knowing why."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pachamama-c27",
      "source_id": "pachamama-s3",
      "quote": "This article analyzes the cult to Andean Mother Earth known as Pachamama, starting from the references contained in those Peruvian chronicles of the sixteenth and seventeenth centuries",
      "locator": "Abstract",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel jurnal ini menganalisis kultus Ibu Bumi Andes yang dikenal sebagai Pachamama berdasarkan rujukan dalam kronik Peru abad keenam belas dan ketujuh belas.",
        "en": "This journal article analyzes the cult of Andean Mother Earth known as Pachamama, starting from references in Peruvian chronicles of the sixteenth and seventeenth centuries."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "pachamama-c28",
      "source_id": "pachamama-s3",
      "quote": "We emphasize, in particular, the peculiarity of the details that they provide about terminology, representations, beliefs and rituals associated with telluric cults in the Prehispanic period, and their persistence throughout the first decades of colonization of the central Andes.",
      "locator": "Abstract",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kronik-kronik itu memberi rincian khas tentang istilah, penggambaran, kepercayaan, dan ritual kultus bumi pada masa pra-Hispanik, serta kelangsungannya selama beberapa dekade pertama kolonisasi Andes tengah.",
        "en": "The chronicles give distinctive details about terminology, representations, beliefs and rituals of the telluric cults of the Prehispanic period, and their persistence through the first decades of colonization of the central Andes."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [
    {
      "topic": {
        "id": "Pasangan dan hubungan keluarga Pachamama",
        "en": "Pachamama's consort and family ties"
      },
      "positions": [
        {
          "summary": {
            "id": "Dalam berbagai mitos ia istri Pachacámac dan ibu Inti serta Mama Quilla.",
            "en": "In various myths she is the wife of Pachacámac and mother of Inti and Mama Quilla."

        {

          },

    "id": "Inti adalah dewa matahari Inca kuno yang dimuliakan sebagai pelindung negara Inca. Ia umumnya dianggap dewa yang murah hati, meski dapat murka.",
    "en": "Inti is the ancient Inca sun god, revered as the national patron of the Inca state. He was generally considered benevolent, although he could be angered.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Inti adalah dewa matahari Inca kuno dan dimuliakan sebagai pelindung negara Inca. Sebagian besar orang menganggapnya dewa matahari, tetapi ia lebih tepat dipandang sebagai gugusan aspek surya, karena bangsa Inca membagi identitasnya menurut tahap-tahap matahari, menjadi tiga subkompleks: ayah, putra, dan saudara. Ia dianggap mahakuasa sekaligus murah hati, namun dapat menunjukkan murkanya lewat gerhana matahari.",
      "en": "Inti is the ancient Inca sun god, held up as patron of the Inca state. Most people regard him as the sun god, yet he is better seen as a cluster of solar aspects, since the Inca split his identity along the stages of the sun into three sub-complexes: father, son and brother. He was thought all-powerful and generous, yet able to show anger through solar eclipses.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "inti-c22",
        "inti-c01",
        "inti-c26",
        "inti-c05",
        "inti-c06"
      ]

    {
      "id": "Menurut kepercayaan yang paling umum, Inti lahir dari Viracocha; sumber lain menyebut Viracocha menciptakan matahari dari sebuah pulau di Danau Titicaca. Inti juga disebut saudara dan, menurut sumber lain, suami Quilla, dewi bulan. Penguasa Inca menganggap diri keturunan langsungnya dan penguasa Inca dipandang sebagai perwakilan hidupnya, sedangkan Manco Capac dipercaya diajari atau malah putranya.",
      "en": "The most common belief was that Inti was born of Viracocha, though another source says Viracocha made the sun from an island on Lake Titicaca. Inti is also called the brother of Quilla, goddess of the moon, and by another source her husband. Inca rulers saw themselves as his direct descendants and the Inca ruler was regarded as his living representative, while Manco Capac was believed to have been taught by him or even to be his son.",

    {
      "id": "Kuil Coricancha di Cuzco dipersembahkan kepada Inti, dan ia dipuja terutama pada festival Inti Raymi. Pachacuti sering dikaitkan dengan asal dan perluasan Kultus Matahari Inca. Salah satu persembahan yang paling umum adalah llama putih. Dalam seni, ia jarang digambarkan; bila ada, wujudnya berupa patung emas, cakram matahari, atau topeng emas, dan salah satu patung menampilkannya sebagai anak kecil duduk bernama Punchao.",
      "en": "The Coricancha temple at Cuzco was dedicated to Inti, and he was worshipped above all at the Inti Raymi festival. Pachacuti is often linked with the origin and spread of the Inca Sun Cult. White llamas were among the commonest offerings.         "inti-c24",
        "inti-c11"
      ]
    },
    {
      "title": {
        "id": "Elang jatuh saat upacara",
        "en": "The falling eagle at the ceremony"
      },
      "role": {
        "id": "Dewa yang dihormati dalam upacara bertanda buruk",
        "en": "Honoured god at an ill-omened ceremony"
      },
      "summary": {
        "id": "Dalam upacara untuk Inti sekitar 1526 M, seekor elang diserang burung nasar kecil dan jatuh dari langit, yang dianggap meramalkan runtuhnya Kekaisaran Inca.",
        "en": "During a ceremony for Inti in c. 1526 CE an eagle was attacked by buzzards and fell from the skies, which was thought to foretell the collapse of the Inca empire."
      },
      "claim_ids": [
        "inti-c18"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "modern_depictions": [],
  "tradition_vs_modern": {
    "traditional": {
      "id": "Pada masa Inca ia dipuja sebagai dewa matahari dan pelindung kekaisaran di kuil seperti Coricancha.",
      "en": "In Inca times he was worshipped as sun god and patron of empire in temples such as Coricancha."
    },
    "modern": {
      "id": "Para pemujanya dan sebagian besar seni atas namanya telah lenyap, tetapi ia hidup dalam budaya masa kini, antara lain lewat Inti Raymi yang dihidupkan kembali di Cuzco.",
      "en": "His worshippers and most of the art made in his name have vanished, but he lives on in present-day cultures, including through the Inti Raymi festival revived in Cuzco."
    },
    "claim_ids": [
      "inti-c13",
      "inti-c19",
      "inti-c20"
    ]
  },
  "learning_questions": [
    {
      "id": "Mengapa penguasa Inca menyatakan diri sebagai keturunan Inti?",
      "en": "Why might Inca rulers have claimed descent from Inti?"
    },
    {
      "id": "Bagaimana Inti Raymi yang dihidupkan kembali menghubungkan masa lalu dan masa kini?",
      "en": "How does the revived Inti Raymi link past and present?"
    }
  ],
  "images": [],
  "sources": [
    {
      "id": "inti-s1",
      "url": "https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Inti",
      "title": "Inti",
      "author": null,
      "publisher": "Wikipedia",
      "published": null,
      "language": "en",
      "type": "wikipedia",
      "accessed": "2026-09-30"
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-s2",
      "url": "https://www.worldhistory.org/Inti/",
      "title": "Inti",
      "author": "Mark Cartwright",
      "publisher": "World History Encyclopedia",
      "published": "2014-03-07",
      "language": "en",
      "type": "encyclopedia",
      "accessed": "2026-10-06"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "inti-c01",
      "source_id": "inti-s1",
      "quote": "Although most consider Inti the sun god, he is more appropriately viewed as a cluster of solar aspects, since the Inca divided his identity according to the stages of the sun.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Inti dipandang sebagai dewa matahari sekaligus klaster aspek surya.",
        "en": "Although most consider Inti the sun god, he is viewed as a cluster of solar aspects."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c02",
      "source_id": "inti-s1",
      "quote": "The most common belief was that Inti was born of Viracocha, who had many titles, chief among them being the God of Creation.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Keyakinan umum menyatakan bahwa Inti dilahirkan oleh Viracocha.",
        "en": "The most common belief was that Inti was born of Viracocha."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c03",
      "source_id": "inti-s1",
      "quote": "Worshiped as a patron deity of the Inca Empire, Pachacuti is often linked to the origin and expansion of the Inca Sun Cult.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dipuja sebagai dewa pelindung kekaisaran, pemujaannya dikaitkan dengan Pachacuti.",
        "en": "Worshiped as a patron deity of the Inca Empire, Pachacuti is linked to his cult."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c04",
      "source_id": "inti-s1",
      "quote": "Inti and his sister, Mama Quilla (also spelled Mama Killa), the Moon goddess were generally considered benevolent deities.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Inti dan saudarinya Mama Quilla umumnya dianggap sebagai dewa yang murah hati.",
        "en": "Inti and his sister, Mama Quilla, were generally considered benevolent deities."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c05",
      "source_id": "inti-s2",
      "quote": "Inti was the Inca god of the sun and considered all-powerful but he was also a benevolent god and capable of great generosity.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Inti adalah dewa matahari Inca yang dianggap mahakuasa, tetapi juga dewa yang murah hati dan mampu bersikap sangat dermawan.",
        "en": "Inti was the Inca god of the sun, considered all-powerful, but also a benevolent god capable of great generosity."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c06",
      "source_id": "inti-s2",
      "quote": "However, Inti could be angered and he would demonstrate his displeasure through solar eclipses which necessitated sacrifices to win back the god's good favour.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Inti dapat murka dan menunjukkan ketidaksenangannya lewat gerhana matahari, yang menuntut persembahan untuk memulihkan kembali kemurahan sang dewa.",
        "en": "Inti could be angered and showed his displeasure through solar eclipses, which called for sacrifices to win back the god's good favour."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c07",
      "source_id": "inti-s2",
      "quote": "Inca rulers considered themselves direct descendants of Inti, the patron of empire and military conquest.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para penguasa Inca menganggap diri keturunan langsung Inti, pelindung kekaisaran dan penaklukan militer.",
        "en": "Inca rulers considered themselves direct descendants of Inti, the patron of empire and military conquest."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c08",
      "source_id": "inti-s2",
      "quote": "Inti was the sun, which in Inca mythology was created by the supreme Inca deity Viracocha from an island on Lake Titicaca.",
      "locator": "Inti's Relations & Role",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Inti adalah matahari, yang dalam mitologi Inca diciptakan oleh dewa tertinggi Inca, Viracocha, dari sebuah pulau di Danau Titicaca.",
        "en": "Inti was the sun, which in Inca mythology was created by the supreme Inca deity Viracocha from an island on Lake Titicaca."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c09",
      "source_id": "inti-s2",
      "quote": "Although Viracocha was higher in status, he usually remained in the background of worldly affairs and allowed intermediaries such as Inti to actually govern the world.",
      "locator": "Inti's Relations & Role",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Meski Viracocha berkedudukan lebih tinggi, ia biasanya tinggal di latar belakang urusan duniawi dan membiarkan perantara seperti Inti benar-benar memerintah dunia.",
        "en": "Although Viracocha was higher in status, he usually remained in the background of worldly affairs and let intermediaries such as Inti actually govern the world."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c10",
      "source_id": "inti-s2",
      "quote": "Inti was married to the goddess of the moon Quilla.",
      "locator": "Inti's Relations & Role",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Inti menikah dengan Quilla, dewi bulan.",
        "en": "Inti was married to the goddess of the moon Quilla."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c11",
      "source_id": "inti-s2",
      "quote": "Manco Capac (previously called Ayar Manco) was believed either to have been instructed by Inti or actually been his son",
      "locator": "Inti's Relations & Role",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Manco Capac (sebelumnya bernama Ayar Manco) dipercaya diajari oleh Inti atau malah putranya.",
        "en": "Manco Capac (previously called Ayar Manco) was believed either to have been instructed by Inti or to have actually been his son."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c12",
      "source_id": "inti-s2",
      "quote": "it was not until Wiraqocha Inca (c. 1425 CE) or the 9th Inca ruler Pachacuti Inca Yupanqui (1438-1471 CE) that the cult of Inti really became established in the cultural practices of the Inca.",
      "locator": "Inti's Relations & Role",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kultus Inti baru benar-benar mapan dalam praktik budaya Inca pada masa Wiraqocha Inca (sekitar 1425 M) atau penguasa Inca ke-9, Pachacuti Inca Yupanqui (1438-1471 M).",
        "en": "The cult of Inti only became really established in Inca cultural practices under Wiraqocha Inca (c. 1425 CE) or the 9th Inca ruler Pachacuti Inca Yupanqui (1438-1471 CE)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c13",
      "source_id": "inti-s2",
      "quote": "The Coricancha temple ('House of the Sun') and Sacsahuaman sacred precinct at the Inca capital of Cuzco were dedicated to Inti.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kuil Coricancha ('Rumah Matahari') dan kawasan suci Sacsahuaman di ibu kota Inca, Cuzco, dipersembahkan kepada Inti.",
        "en": "The Coricancha temple ('House of the Sun') and the Sacsahuaman sacred precinct at the Inca capital Cuzco were dedicated to Inti."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c14",
      "source_id": "inti-s2",
      "quote": "Perhaps the most famous example is at Machu Picchu where a typical intihuatana or 'hitching post of the sun' situated at the highest point of the sacred precinct was used during the solstices to symbolically connect the sun to the earth using a special cord.",
      "locator": "Worship",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Contoh paling terkenal ada di Machu Picchu, tempat intihuatana atau 'tiang penambat matahari' di titik tertinggi kawasan suci dipakai pada titik balik matahari untuk menghubungkan matahari dengan bumi secara simbolis memakai tali khusus.",
        "en": "Perhaps the most famous example is at Machu Picchu, where an intihuatana or 'hitching post of the sun' at the highest point of the sacred precinct was used during the solstices to connect the sun symbolically to the earth with a special cord."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c15",
      "source_id": "inti-s2",
      "quote": "Inti was especially worshipped during the festival of Inti Raymi held during the winter solstice in June and run over several days. One of the most common sacrifices to Inti was of white llamas",
      "locator": "Inti Raymi",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Inti terutama dipuja pada festival Inti Raymi yang digelar pada titik balik matahari musim dingin bulan Juni selama beberapa hari; salah satu persembahan paling umum adalah llama putih.",
        "en": "Inti was especially worshipped during the Inti Raymi festival, held at the winter solstice in June over several days; one of the most common sacrifices was white llamas."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c16",
      "source_id": "inti-s2",
      "quote": "Inti was not often represented in Inca art but when he was it was usually as a gold statue, a sun disk or a golden mask. Gold was particularly associated with the god as it was believed that the precious metal was the sweat of the sun.",
      "locator": "Representations in Art",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Inti jarang digambarkan dalam seni Inca, dan bila digambarkan biasanya sebagai patung emas, cakram matahari, atau topeng emas; emas dikaitkan dengan sang dewa karena dipercaya sebagai keringat matahari.",
        "en": "Inti was not often represented in Inca art, and when he was it was usually as a gold statue, a sun disk or a golden mask; gold was associated with the god because it was believed to be the sweat of the sun."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c17",
      "source_id": "inti-s2",
      "quote": "The statue represented Inti as a small seated boy, called Punchao (Day or Midday Sun).",
      "locator": "Representations in Art",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Patung itu menggambarkan Inti sebagai anak kecil yang duduk, bernama Punchao (Matahari Siang atau Tengah Hari).",
        "en": "The statue represented Inti as a small seated boy, called Punchao (Day or Midday Sun)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c18",
      "source_id": "inti-s2",
      "quote": "One curious story is when an eagle was attacked by buzzards and fell from the skies during a ceremony in honour of Inti held in c. 1526 CE which was thought to foretell the collapse of the Inca empire.",
      "locator": "Inti Raymi",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam satu kisah, seekor elang diserang burung nasar kecil dan jatuh dari langit saat upacara untuk Inti sekitar 1526 M, yang dianggap meramalkan runtuhnya Kekaisaran Inca.",
        "en": "In one story an eagle was attacked by buzzards and fell from the skies during a ceremony in honour of Inti in c. 1526 CE, which was thought to foretell the collapse of the Inca empire."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c19",
      "source_id": "inti-s2",
      "quote": "The god's worshippers and the vast majority of the art made in his name have long since disappeared but Inti lives on in several present day South American cultures.",
      "locator": "Representations in Art",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para pemuja sang dewa dan sebagian besar seni yang dibuat atas namanya telah lama lenyap, tetapi Inti masih hidup dalam beberapa budaya Amerika Selatan masa kini.",
        "en": "The god's worshippers and most of the art made in his name have long since disappeared, but Inti lives on in several present-day South American cultures."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c20",
      "source_id": "inti-s2",
      "quote": "The festival of Inti Raymi has been revived as a great yearly festival in Cuzco, several national flags bear an image of him in the form of a flaming sun",
      "locator": "Representations in Art",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Festival Inti Raymi dihidupkan kembali sebagai festival tahunan besar di Cuzco, dan beberapa bendera nasional memuat gambarnya berupa matahari yang menyala.",
        "en": "The Inti Raymi festival has been revived as a great yearly festival in Cuzco, and several national flags bear an image of him as a flaming sun."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c21",
      "source_id": "inti-s2",
      "quote": "Other important temples were at Pisac (north-east of Cuzco), Ingapirca (in modern Ecuador), and on the Island of the Sun on Lake Titicaca which Inca rulers visited once a year in pilgrimage.",
      "locator": "Worship",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kuil penting lain berada di Pisac, Ingapirca (di Ekuador masa kini), dan di Pulau Matahari di Danau Titicaca, yang dikunjungi para penguasa Inca setahun sekali dalam ziarah.",
        "en": "Other important temples were at Pisac, Ingapirca (in modern Ecuador) and on the Island of the Sun on Lake Titicaca, which Inca rulers visited once a year in pilgrimage."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c22",
      "source_id": "inti-s1",
      "quote": "Inti (Quechua: inti, lit. 'sun' ) is the ancient Inca sun god. He is revered as the national patron of the Inca state.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Inti (Quechua: inti, secara harfiah 'matahari') adalah dewa matahari Inca kuno, dimuliakan sebagai pelindung nasional negara Inca.",
        "en": "Inti (Quechua: inti, literally 'sun') is the ancient Inca sun god, revered as the national patron of the Inca state."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c23",
      "source_id": "inti-s1",
      "quote": "The word inti is not of Quechua origin but a loanword from Puquina.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kata inti bukan berasal dari bahasa Quechua, melainkan kata serapan dari bahasa Puquina.",
        "en": "The word inti is not of Quechua origin but a loanword from Puquina."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c24",
      "source_id": "inti-s1",
      "quote": "According to myth, Inti taught Manco Cápac and his daughter Mama Ocllo the arts of civilization.",
      "locator": "Legends and history",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut mitos, Inti mengajarkan seni peradaban kepada Manco Cápac dan putrinya, Mama Ocllo.",
        "en": "According to myth, Inti taught Manco Cápac and his daughter Mama Ocllo the arts of civilization."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c25",
      "source_id": "inti-s1",
      "quote": "The sun was also important to the Incas, particularly the people of the highlands, because it was necessary for the production of crops like maize and other grains. The sun's heat was also thought to cause rain.",
      "locator": "Worship",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Matahari penting bagi bangsa Inca, terutama penduduk dataran tinggi, karena diperlukan untuk menghasilkan jagung dan biji-bijian lain; panas matahari juga dianggap menyebabkan hujan.",
        "en": "The sun was important to the Incas, particularly highland people, because it was necessary for producing maize and other grains; the sun's heat was also thought to cause rain."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c26",
      "source_id": "inti-s1",
      "quote": "Inti's identity is also divided into three primary subcomplexes, which are the father, son, and brother.",
      "locator": "Sub-divisions of identity",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Identitas Inti juga dibagi menjadi tiga subkompleks utama, yaitu ayah, putra, dan saudara.",
        "en": "Inti's identity is also divided into three primary subcomplexes: the father, the son and the brother."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c27",
      "source_id": "inti-s1",
      "quote": "The first of these is Apu Inti (\"supreme Inti\"). He represents the father and is sometimes known as \"The Lord Sun\". The second is Churi Inti, or \"Son Inti\", who represents the son of Inti and is often known as \"Daylight\". The third and final division of Inti is Inti Wawqi (\"Sun brother\", or \"Inti brother\", also spelled Inti-Guauqui, Inti-Huaoqui).",
      "locator": "Sub-divisions of identity",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ketiganya adalah Apu Inti (\"Inti tertinggi\"), Churi Inti (\"Inti putra\", sering disebut \"Cahaya Siang\"), dan Inti Wawqi (\"saudara matahari\").",
        "en": "The three are Apu Inti (\"supreme Inti\"), Churi Inti (\"Son Inti\", often known as \"Daylight\") and Inti Wawqi (\"Sun brother\")."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c28",
      "source_id": "inti-s1",
      "quote": "Some sources identify the central figure of the Gateway of the Sun as Inti and others as Viracocha",
      "locator": "Legends and history",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagian sumber mengidentifikasi tokoh utama pada Gerbang Matahari sebagai Inti dan sebagian lain sebagai Viracocha.",
        "en": "Some sources identify the central figure of the Gateway of the Sun as Inti and others as Viracocha."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "inti-c29",
      "source_id": "inti-s1",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Mothman adalah kriptid humanoid dalam cerita rakyat Amerika yang konon terlihat di sekitar Point Pleasant, West Virginia, pada 1966 hingga 1967. Meski bernama \"ngengat\", laporan awalnya menggambarkan ciri burung.",
    "en": "Mothman is a humanoid cryptid of American folklore reportedly seen around Point Pleasant, West Virginia, in 1966 and 1967. Despite the name, the first reports described bird-like rather than moth-like features.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Mothman adalah nama untuk kriptid humanoid yang konon terlihat di sekitar Point Pleasant, West Virginia, dari 15 November 1966 hingga 15 Desember 1967. Laporan surat kabar pertama terbit pada 16 November 1966, dan pers nasional segera mengangkatnya sehingga kisah ini menyebar ke seluruh Amerika Serikat. Meski bernama Mothman, laporan awal menggambarkan ciri burung, bukan ngengat.",
      "en": "Mothman is the name for a humanoid cryptid reportedly seen around Point Pleasant, West Virginia, between November 15, 1966 and December 15, 1967. The first newspaper report appeared on November 16, 1966, and the national press soon took it up, spreading the story across the United States. Despite its name, the earliest reports described features of a bird, not a moth.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "mothman-c05",
        "mothman-c06",
        "mothman-c02",
        "mothman-c01"

    {
      "id": "Pada 15 November 1966 dua pasangan muda mengaku melihat makhluk hitam besar bermata menyala merah di dekat \"area TNT\", bekas pabrik amunisi. Linda Scarberry menggambarkannya sebagai lelaki langsing berotot setinggi sekitar tujuh kaki dengan sayap putih. Para saksi melaporkan bahwa makhluk itu terbang dan mengimbangi mobil mereka. Laporan lain menyebut sosok bersayap besar bermata merah menyala yang terpusat di sekitar Point Pleasant. Burung hantu barred memiliki pantulan cahaya mata merah tua yang mencolok, yang menurut satu sumber mungkin menjelaskan mata merah itu.",
      "en": "On November 15, 1966 two young couples said they saw a large black creature with glowing red eyes near the \"TNT area\", the site of an old munitions plant. In Linda Scarberry's account the figure was a lean, muscular man roughly seven feet tall, with white wings. The witnesses reported that it took flight and kept pace with their car. Other accounts speak of a large winged figure with glowing red eyes centred on the Point Pleasant area. One source notes that barred owls have a vivid crimson eyeshine, which may explain the red eyes.",

      "id": "Setelah Silver Bridge runtuh pada 15 Desember 1967 dan 46 orang tewas, muncul legenda yang menghubungkan penampakan itu dengan runtuhnya jembatan, dan penampakan menurun sesudahnya. John Keel mempopulerkan kaitan itu dalam bukunya tahun 1975.       "en": "After the Silver Bridge collapsed on December 15, 1967 and 46 people died, a legend arose tying the sightings to the collapse, and sightings diminished afterward.     {
      "id": "mothman-s3",
      "url": "https://www.audubon.org/magazine/mothman-of-west-virginia-owl",
      "title": "Is the Mothman of West Virginia an Owl?",
      "author": "Asher Elbein",
      "publisher": "Audubon magazine (National Audubon Society)",

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "mothman-c01",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "Despite its name, the original sightings of the creature described avian, not lepidopteran, features.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Meskipun dinamai Mothman, penampakan awal makhluk ini menggambarkan ciri burung dan bukan serangga.",
        "en": "Despite its name, original sightings of the creature described avian, not lepidopteran, features."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c02",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "The national press soon picked up the reports and helped spread the story across the United States.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pers nasional segera mengangkat laporan itu dan membantu menyebarkan kisahnya ke seluruh Amerika Serikat.",
        "en": "The national press soon picked up the reports and helped spread the story across the United States."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c03",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "The creature was later popularized by John Keel in his 1975 book The Mothman Prophecies, which claimed that there were paranormal events related to the sightings and a connection to the collapse of the Silver Bridge.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk ini dipopulerkan oleh John Keel dalam bukunya The Mothman Prophecies yang mengaitkannya dengan runtuhnya Silver Bridge.",
        "en": "The creature was popularized by John Keel in his book The Mothman Prophecies connecting it to the collapse of the Silver Bridge."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c04",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "An annual festival in Point Pleasant is devoted to the Mothman legend.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Festival tahunan di Point Pleasant didedikasikan untuk legenda Mothman.",
        "en": "An annual festival in Point Pleasant is devoted to the Mothman legend."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c05",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "In American folklore, Mothman is the name given to a humanoid cryptid that was reportedly seen around Point Pleasant, West Virginia, from November 15, 1966, to December 15, 1967.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Amerika, Mothman adalah nama untuk kriptid humanoid yang konon terlihat di sekitar Point Pleasant, West Virginia, dari 15 November 1966 hingga 15 Desember 1967.",
        "en": "In American folklore, Mothman is the name given to a humanoid cryptid reportedly seen around Point Pleasant, West Virginia, from November 15, 1966 to December 15, 1967."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c06",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "The first newspaper report was published in the Point Pleasant Register, dated November 16, 1966, titled \"Couples See Man-Sized Bird ... Creature ... Something\".",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Laporan surat kabar pertama terbit di Point Pleasant Register tertanggal 16 November 1966 dengan judul \"Couples See Man-Sized Bird ... Creature ... Something\".",
        "en": "The first newspaper report appeared in the Point Pleasant Register, dated November 16, 1966, titled \"Couples See Man-Sized Bird ... Creature ... Something\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c07",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "On November 15, 1966, two young couples from Point Pleasant-Roger and Linda Scarberry, Steve and Mary Mallette-told police they had seen a large black creature whose eyes \"glowed red\", standing at the side of the road near \"the TNT area\", the site of a former munitions plant from World War II.",
      "locator": "History",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 15 November 1966, dua pasangan muda dari Point Pleasant, Roger dan Linda Scarberry serta Steve dan Mary Mallette, memberi tahu polisi bahwa mereka melihat makhluk hitam besar bermata \"menyala merah\" di tepi jalan dekat \"area TNT\", bekas pabrik amunisi Perang Dunia II.",
        "en": "On November 15, 1966, two young couples from Point Pleasant, Roger and Linda Scarberry and Steve and Mary Mallette, told police they had seen a large black creature whose eyes \"glowed red\" at the side of the road near \"the TNT area\", the site of a former World War II munitions plant."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c08",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "Linda Scarberry described it as a \"slender, muscular man\" about seven feet (2.1 m) tall with white wings. However, she was unable to discern its face due to the hypnotic effect of its eyes.",
      "locator": "History",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Linda Scarberry menggambarkannya sebagai \"lelaki langsing berotot\" setinggi sekitar tujuh kaki dengan sayap putih, tetapi ia tidak dapat melihat wajahnya karena efek hipnotis dari matanya.",
        "en": "Linda Scarberry described it as a \"slender, muscular man\" about seven feet tall with white wings, but she could not make out its face because of the hypnotic effect of its eyes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c09",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "Distressed, the witnesses claimed they sped away at 100 miles per hour (160 km/h), reporting that the creature took flight and kept pace with their car, making a screeching sound during the pursuit.",
      "locator": "History",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para saksi mengaku melaju kabur dengan kecepatan 100 mil per jam, dan melaporkan bahwa makhluk itu terbang dan mengimbangi mobil mereka sambil mengeluarkan suara melengking selama pengejaran.",
        "en": "The witnesses claimed they sped away at 100 miles per hour, reporting that the creature took flight and kept pace with their car, making a screeching sound during the pursuit."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c10",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "Newell Partridge told Johnson that when he aimed a flashlight at a creature in a nearby field, its eyes glowed \"like bicycle reflectors\".",
      "locator": "History",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Newell Partridge mengatakan bahwa ketika ia menyorotkan senter ke makhluk di ladang terdekat, matanya menyala \"seperti reflektor sepeda\".",
        "en": "Newell Partridge said that when he aimed a flashlight at a creature in a nearby field, its eyes glowed \"like bicycle reflectors\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c11",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "The source of the legend is believed to have originated from sightings of out-of-migration sandhill cranes or herons, or possibly the misidentification of barred owls.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sumber legenda ini diyakini berasal dari penampakan bangau sandhill yang tersesat dari jalur migrasi atau bangau, atau mungkin salah identifikasi burung hantu barred.",
        "en": "The source of the legend is believed to have originated from sightings of out-of-migration sandhill cranes or herons, or possibly the misidentification of barred owls."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c12",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "While the villain Killer Moth did not appear in the show, the comic book influence of both him and Batman is believed to have influenced the coinage of the name \"Mothman\" in the local newspapers.",
      "locator": "History",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pengaruh komik Batman dan penjahat Killer Moth diyakini memengaruhi pemberian nama \"Mothman\" di surat kabar lokal.",
        "en": "The comic book influence of Batman and the villain Killer Moth is believed to have influenced the coinage of the name \"Mothman\" in local newspapers."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c13",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "Following the December 15, 1967, collapse of the Silver Bridge and the deaths of 46 people, the incident gave rise to a legend that connected the Mothman sightings to the bridge collapse.",
      "locator": "History",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Setelah runtuhnya Silver Bridge pada 15 Desember 1967 yang menewaskan 46 orang, muncul legenda yang menghubungkan penampakan Mothman dengan runtuhnya jembatan itu.",
        "en": "After the collapse of the Silver Bridge on December 15, 1967 and the deaths of 46 people, a legend arose connecting the Mothman sightings to the bridge collapse."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c14",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "More speculative hypotheses (such as those proposed by ufologists, paranormal authors, and cryptozoologists) suggest the Mothman could be an alien, a supernatural manifestation, or a previously unknown species of animal.",
      "locator": "Analysis",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hipotesis yang lebih spekulatif (misalnya dari ufolog, penulis paranormal, dan kriptozoolog) menyebut Mothman bisa jadi alien, wujud supranatural, atau spesies hewan yang belum dikenal.",
        "en": "More speculative hypotheses (such as those of ufologists, paranormal authors and cryptozoologists) suggest the Mothman could be an alien, a supernatural manifestation or a previously unknown species of animal."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c15",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "Nickell attributes the Mothman stories to misidentifications of barred owls, suggesting that the Mothman's \"glowing eyes\" were actually the phenomenon of eyeshine, common in many animals",
      "locator": "Analysis",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nickell mengaitkan kisah Mothman dengan salah identifikasi burung hantu barred, dan menduga \"mata menyala\" Mothman sebenarnya fenomena pantulan cahaya mata (eyeshine) yang umum pada banyak hewan.",
        "en": "Nickell attributes the Mothman stories to misidentifications of barred owls, suggesting the \"glowing eyes\" were actually eyeshine, a phenomenon common in many animals."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c16",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "Brunvand found elements in common among many Mothman reports and much older folktales, suggesting that something real may have triggered the scares and become interwoven with existing folklore.",
      "locator": "Analysis",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Brunvand menemukan unsur yang sama antara banyak laporan Mothman dan dongeng yang jauh lebih tua, yang mengisyaratkan bahwa sesuatu yang nyata mungkin memicu kepanikan dan menyatu dengan cerita rakyat yang ada.",
        "en": "Brunvand found elements in common between many Mothman reports and much older folktales, suggesting that something real may have triggered the scares and become interwoven with existing folklore."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c17",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "In his 1975 book, Keel claimed that Point Pleasant residents experienced precognitions, including premonitions of the collapse of the Silver Bridge, UFO sightings, visits from inhuman or threatening men in black, and other alleged phenomena.",
      "locator": "Analysis",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam bukunya tahun 1975, Keel mengklaim penduduk Point Pleasant mengalami prekognisi, termasuk firasat runtuhnya Silver Bridge, penampakan UFO, kunjungan pria tak manusiawi atau mengancam (men in black), dan fenomena lain yang diklaim.",
        "en": "In his 1975 book Keel claimed Point Pleasant residents experienced precognitions, including premonitions of the collapse of the Silver Bridge, UFO sightings, visits from inhuman or threatening men in black, and other alleged phenomena."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c18",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "The Mothman legend has become a major source of tourism for Point Pleasant.",
      "locator": "Festival and statues",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Legenda Mothman telah menjadi sumber utama pariwisata bagi Point Pleasant.",
        "en": "The Mothman legend has become a major source of tourism for Point Pleasant."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c19",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "A 12-foot-tall (3.7 m) metallic statue of the creature, created by artist and sculptor Bob Roach, was unveiled in 2003. The Mothman Museum and Research Center opened in 2006.",
      "locator": "Festival and statues",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Patung logam setinggi 12 kaki karya seniman dan pematung Bob Roach diresmikan pada 2003, dan Mothman Museum and Research Center dibuka pada 2006.",
        "en": "A 12-foot-tall metallic statue of the creature, by artist and sculptor Bob Roach, was unveiled in 2003, and the Mothman Museum and Research Center opened in 2006."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c20",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "The city held its first Annual Mothman Festival in 2002.",
      "locator": "Festival and statues",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kota itu menyelenggarakan Festival Mothman tahunan yang pertama pada 2002.",
        "en": "The city held its first Annual Mothman Festival in 2002."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c21",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "The Mothman Prophecies was adapted decades later into a 2002 film starring Richard Gere.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "The Mothman Prophecies diadaptasi beberapa dekade kemudian menjadi film tahun 2002 yang dibintangi Richard Gere.",
        "en": "The Mothman Prophecies was adapted decades later into a 2002 film starring Richard Gere."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c22",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "The 2018 video game Fallout 76 features the Mothman as a central figure",
      "locator": "In popular culture",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Gim video Fallout 76 (2018) menampilkan Mothman sebagai tokoh sentral.",
        "en": "The 2018 video game Fallout 76 features the Mothman as a central figure."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c23",
      "source_id": "mothman-s1",
      "quote": "Small Town Monsters has released two documentaries about Mothman, The Mothman of Point Pleasant in 2017 and The Mothman Legacy in 2020.",
      "locator": "In popular culture",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Small Town Monsters merilis dua film dokumenter tentang Mothman: The Mothman of Point Pleasant (2017) dan The Mothman Legacy (2020).",
        "en": "Small Town Monsters has released two documentaries about Mothman, The Mothman of Point Pleasant in 2017 and The Mothman Legacy in 2020."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c24",
      "source_id": "mothman-s2",
      "quote": "Mothman is one of several legendary West Virginia monsters, along with Bat Boy and the Flatwoods Monster. Persistent sightings began in November 1966, and totaled 26 over a one-year span.",
      "locator": "Mothman",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mothman adalah salah satu dari beberapa monster legendaris West Virginia, bersama Bat Boy dan Flatwoods Monster; penampakan bertahan dimulai November 1966 dan berjumlah 26 dalam setahun.",
        "en": "Mothman is one of several legendary West Virginia monsters, along with Bat Boy and the Flatwoods Monster; persistent sightings began in November 1966 and totalled 26 over a one-year span."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c25",
      "source_id": "mothman-s2",
      "quote": "The reports of a large, winged creature with glowing red eyes were centered in the Point Pleasant area, usually in or near a vast, abandoned munitions facility known as the TNT plant.",
      "locator": "Mothman",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Laporan tentang makhluk bersayap besar bermata merah menyala terpusat di sekitar Point Pleasant, biasanya di dalam atau dekat fasilitas amunisi terbengkalai yang luas yang dikenal sebagai pabrik TNT.",
        "en": "Reports of a large, winged creature with glowing red eyes were centered in the Point Pleasant area, usually in or near a vast abandoned munitions facility known as the TNT plant."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c26",
      "source_id": "mothman-s2",
      "quote": "As sightings increased, so did reports of other strange phenomena. Disruption of electronic devices such as telephones, police dispatch radios, and televisions, as well as automobiles, were rumored, and there were numerous reports of UFO appearances.",
      "locator": "Mothman",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seiring bertambahnya penampakan, bertambah pula laporan fenomena aneh lain; gangguan pada telepon, radio polisi, televisi, dan mobil dikabarkan terjadi, serta banyak laporan kemunculan UFO.",
        "en": "As sightings increased, so did reports of other strange phenomena; disruption of telephones, police dispatch radios, televisions and automobiles was rumored, along with numerous UFO reports."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c27",
      "source_id": "mothman-s2",
      "quote": "In one popular theory, the Mothman's advent and the subsequent Silver Bridge disaster were linked to a 200-year-old curse attributed to Shawnee Chief Cornstalk who led the Indians at the Battle Point of Pleasant and who later was murdered there.",
      "locator": "Mothman",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam satu teori populer, kedatangan Mothman dan bencana Silver Bridge berikutnya dikaitkan dengan kutukan berusia 200 tahun yang dikaitkan dengan Kepala Suku Shawnee Cornstalk, yang memimpin pasukan di Battle Point of Pleasant dan kemudian dibunuh di sana.",
        "en": "In one popular theory, the Mothman's advent and the subsequent Silver Bridge disaster were linked to a 200-year-old curse attributed to Shawnee Chief Cornstalk, who led his people at the Battle of Point Pleasant and was later murdered there."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c28",
      "source_id": "mothman-s2",
      "quote": "Sightings of the Mothman diminished after the Silver Bridge collapse.",
      "locator": "Mothman",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penampakan Mothman menurun setelah runtuhnya Silver Bridge.",
        "en": "Sightings of the Mothman diminished after the Silver Bridge collapse."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c29",
      "source_id": "mothman-s2",
      "quote": "Beginning in 2011, and as recently as 2020, a Mothman-like figure has been seen numerous times in the Chicago area.",
      "locator": "Mothman",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sejak 2011 hingga sedini 2020, sosok mirip Mothman dilaporkan terlihat berkali-kali di kawasan Chicago.",
        "en": "From 2011 and as recently as 2020, a Mothman-like figure has been seen numerous times in the Chicago area."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c30",
      "source_id": "mothman-s2",
      "quote": "Young Breece D'J Pancake, before he became celebrated as a short story writer, published a 1966 letter in the Huntington Herald Dispatch arguing that Mothman was in fact a California condor.",
      "locator": "Mothman",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Breece D'J Pancake muda menerbitkan surat tahun 1966 di Huntington Herald Dispatch yang berargumen bahwa Mothman sebenarnya burung kondor California.",
        "en": "The young Breece D'J Pancake published a 1966 letter in the Huntington Herald Dispatch arguing that Mothman was in fact a California condor."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c31",
      "source_id": "mothman-s3",
      "quote": "There the headlights glanced off something in the dark: a six-foot-tall, man-shaped thing. Shining red eyes stared back at them from a body without an obvious head or neck.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lampu depan mobil menyorot sesuatu di kegelapan: benda berbentuk manusia setinggi enam kaki, dengan mata merah menyala yang menatap dari tubuh tanpa kepala atau leher yang jelas.",
        "en": "The headlights glanced off something in the dark: a six-foot-tall, man-shaped thing with shining red eyes staring from a body without an obvious head or neck."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c32",
      "source_id": "mothman-s3",
      "quote": "Spooked, the couples sped away, only to see the thing following them on silent, unmoving wings.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ketakutan, pasangan itu kabur, tetapi melihat benda itu mengikuti mereka dengan sayap yang senyap dan tak bergerak.",
        "en": "Spooked, the couples sped away, only to see the thing following them on silent, unmoving wings."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c33",
      "source_id": "mothman-s3",
      "quote": "Yet according to Joe Nickell, an author and investigator with the Committee for Skeptical Inquiry, the original Mothman likely owes its existence to a more mundane-though no less interesting-culprit: an owl.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Joe Nickell, penulis dan penyelidik Committee for Skeptical Inquiry, Mothman asli kemungkinan besar berasal dari penyebab yang lebih biasa namun tak kalah menarik: seekor burung hantu.",
        "en": "According to Joe Nickell, an author and investigator with the Committee for Skeptical Inquiry, the original Mothman likely owes its existence to a more mundane yet no less interesting culprit: an owl."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c34",
      "source_id": "mothman-s3",
      "quote": "Barred Owls have a dramatic crimson eyeshine due to the amount of blood vessels around the eye.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Burung hantu Barred memiliki pantulan cahaya mata merah tua yang mencolok karena banyaknya pembuluh darah di sekitar mata.",
        "en": "Barred Owls have a dramatic crimson eyeshine due to the amount of blood vessels around the eye."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c35",
      "source_id": "mothman-s3",
      "quote": "Paranormal writers played up the otherworldly aspects of the sightings, reinterpreting and reshaping the legend to suit their needs. Current artistic interpretations of Mothman often cast it as demonic or, more understandably, insect-like.",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penulis paranormal menonjolkan sisi gaib dari penampakan itu dan membentuk ulang legendanya; tafsir artistik masa kini sering menggambarkan Mothman sebagai iblis atau, yang lebih masuk akal, mirip serangga.",
        "en": "Paranormal writers played up the otherworldly aspects of the sightings and reshaped the legend; current artistic interpretations often cast Mothman as demonic or, more understandably, insect-like."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mothman-c36",
      "source_id": "mothman-s3",
      "quote": "Nickell grew interested in the Mothman case after looking into the similar Flatwoods Monster",
      "locator": "Article text",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nickell tertarik pada kasus Mothman setelah menyelidiki Flatwoods Monster yang serupa.",
        "en": "Nickell grew interested in the Mothman case after looking into the similar Flatwoods Monster."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [
    {
      "topic": {
        "id": "Apa yang sebenarnya dilihat para saksi",
        "en": "What the witnesses actually saw"
      },
      "positions": [
        {
          "summary": {

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Wendigo adalah makhluk mitologis atau roh jahat dari cerita rakyat Algonquian, dipandang sebagai makhluk gaib pemakan manusia yang dikaitkan dengan musim dingin dan kelaparan.",
    "en": "The wendigo is a mythological creature or evil spirit of Algonquian folklore, seen as a cannibalistic supernatural being linked with winter and famine.",
    "claim_ids": [

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Wendigo adalah bagian dari sistem kepercayaan tradisional beberapa bangsa penutur bahasa Algonquian, termasuk Ojibwe, Saulteaux, Cree, Naskapi, dan Innu. Ia sering dikatakan roh jahat, kadang digambarkan sebagai makhluk bersifat manusiawi, yang dapat merasuki manusia. Pada semua budaya itu ia dipandang sebagai makhluk gaib yang jahat dan kanibal, sangat dikaitkan dengan utara, musim dingin, kelaparan, dan paceklik. Sumber lain menyebutnya monster rakus Bangsa-Bangsa Pertama Kanada dan budaya Indian Dataran yang muncul dengan nama lain dalam kisah banyak bangsa Pribumi Amerika.",
      "en": "The wendigo belongs to the traditional belief system of several Algonquian-speaking peoples, among them the Ojibwe, Saulteaux, Cree, Naskapi and Innu. It is often called a malevolent spirit, sometimes pictured with human-like traits, that can possess people. Across these cultures it is seen as an evil, cannibalistic supernatural being tied closely to the north, winter and famine. Another source calls it a voracious monster of the First Nations of Canada and Plains Indian culture, found under other names in many Native American nations.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "wendigo-c01",
        "wendigo-c02",
        "wendigo-c08",
        "wendigo-c33"
      ]

    {
      "id": "Korbannya dikatakan merasakan lapar yang tak terpuaskan, keinginan memakan manusia lain, dan kecenderungan membunuh. Dalam sebagian gambaran, wendigo adalah humanoid raksasa berhati es yang kedatangannya ditandai bau busuk atau hawa dingin tak lazim. Dalam kisah Ojibwe, Cree, Naskapi, dan Innu, ia sering digambarkan raksasa yang membesar sebanding dengan santapannya sehingga tak pernah kenyang.",
      "en": "Its victims are said to feel insatiable hunger, a wish to eat other humans, and a tendency to murder. In some depictions the wendigo is a giant humanoid with a heart of ice whose approach is marked by a foul stench or an unseasonable chill. In Ojibwe, Cree, Naskapi and Innu lore it is often a giant that swells with each meal, so it is never full.",

      "id": "Mengenai asalnya, dalam sebagian tradisi manusia yang dikuasai keserakahan dapat menjadi wendigo, sedangkan sumber lain menyebut wendigo tercipta ketika seseorang memakan sesamanya demi bertahan hidup.       "en": "On its origin, in some traditions humans overcome by greed could become wendigos, while other sources say a wendigo arose when someone turned to cannibalism to survive.       "en": "Among the Assiniboine, Cree and Ojibwe, a satirical ceremonial dance is sometimes held in times of famine to underline the wendigo taboo.
